<script id="ethvelle-v91-interaction-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  /* Put Key and Zoom beside the transport cluster, not inside it. The Previous / Play /
     Next cluster can then stay centered exactly on the blue playhead while these two tools
     sit at the far right edge of the same row. */
  function dockTransportUtilityButtons(){
    const row=document.querySelector('#screen-editor > .transport-row');if(!row)return;
    const key=$('transportKeyBtn'),zoom=$('transportZoomBtn');
    if(key&&key.parentElement!==row)row.appendChild(key);
    if(zoom&&zoom.parentElement!==row)row.appendChild(zoom);
  }
  dockTransportUtilityButtons();
  requestAnimationFrame(dockTransportUtilityButtons);

  /* 1) Transform ruler: map any horizontal touch on the ruler to the range's value.
     This bypasses the global thumb-only slider hit area that made the ruler feel stuck. */
  function bindTransformRuler(ruler){
    if(!ruler||ruler.dataset.evV91Bound==='1')return;
    const input=ruler.querySelector('input[type="range"]');if(!input)return;
    ruler.dataset.evV91Bound='1';
    input.style.setProperty('pointer-events','none','important');
    input.style.setProperty('touch-action','none','important');
    ruler.style.setProperty('touch-action','none','important');
    let drag=null,raf=0,pendingX=0;
    function setFromClientX(x,emit){
      if(!drag)return;
      const rect=ruler.getBoundingClientRect();if(!rect.width)return;
      const usable=Math.max(1,rect.width-20);
      const min=drag.min,max=drag.max,step=drag.step;
      /* The finger drags the grey ruler; the blue centre marker never moves. */
      let value=drag.startValue+((drag.startX-x)/usable)*(max-min);
      if(step>0)value=min+Math.round((value-min)/step)*step;
      value=Math.max(min,Math.min(max,value));
      const decimals=(String(input.step||'1').split('.')[1]||'').length;
      const next=String(Number(value.toFixed(Math.min(6,decimals))));
      if(String(input.value)!==next){input.value=next;input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));}
      if(emit)input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
    }
    function flush(){if(!drag)return;if(raf){cancelAnimationFrame(raf);raf=0;}setFromClientX(pendingX,false);}
    ruler.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      if(e.isPrimary===false||(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0))return;
      const target=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('button'):null;if(target)return;
      const min=Number.isFinite(Number(input.min))?Number(input.min):0;
      const max=Number.isFinite(Number(input.max))?Number(input.max):100;
      drag={id:e.pointerId,startX:e.clientX,startValue:Number(input.value)||0,min,max,step:input.step==='any'?0:(Number(input.step)||1)};
      pendingX=e.clientX;
      try{ruler.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
      ruler.classList.add('evtr-dragging');
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    },{passive:false});
    ruler.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      if(!drag||drag.id!==e.pointerId)return;
      pendingX=e.clientX;
      if(!raf)raf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{raf=0;if(drag)setFromClientX(pendingX,false);});
      if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    },{passive:false});
    const finish=e=>{
      if(!drag||e.pointerId!==drag.id)return;
      flush();input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));drag=null;
      ruler.classList.remove('evtr-dragging');
      try{ruler.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    };
    ruler.addEventListener('pointerup',finish,{passive:false});
    ruler.addEventListener('pointercancel',finish,{passive:false});
    ruler.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',()=>{drag=null;ruler.classList.remove('evtr-dragging');if(raf){cancelAnimationFrame(raf);raf=0;}},{passive:true});
  }
  function scanTransformRulers(root){
    if(!root)return;
    if(root.nodeType===1&&root.matches?.('.evtr-ruler'))bindTransformRuler(root);
    root.querySelectorAll?.('.evtr-ruler').forEach(bindTransformRuler);
  }
  scanTransformRulers(document);
  const panel=$('toolPanel');
  if(panel&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    new MutationObserver(records=>records.forEach(record=>record.addedNodes.forEach(node=>{if(node.nodeType===1)scanTransformRulers(node);}))).observe(panel,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  const oldOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function'&&!oldOpen.__evv91Rulers){
    window.openPanel=function(key){const result=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='transform')requestAnimationFrame(()=>scanTransformRulers($('toolPanel')));return result;};
    window.openPanel.__evv91Rulers=true;
  }

  /* 2) RAF-driven bottom-tool scrolling. A normal tap is never cancelled, so tool buttons
     remain clickable. Only a clearly horizontal drag takes ownership of the gesture. */
  function bindBottomToolbar(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');
    if(!row)return;
    // Intentionally no JS pointer listeners: native overflow scrolling has momentum,
    // preserves clicks, and avoids a per-pointermove JavaScript scroll loop on Android.
    row.dataset.evV91Scroll='native';
  }
  bindBottomToolbar();

  /* 3) Playhead contact is actual keyframe selection, not only a temporary red highlight.
     The sync runs after the canonical clock/render updates and inspects only the active row. */
  function timelineTime(){
    try{if(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function')return Number(getCurrentAbsoluteTime());}catch(_e){}
    return Number(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
  }
  function setMarkerState(track,selector,activeIndex,contactIndex){
    if(!track)return;
    const markers=track.querySelectorAll('.kf-marker');
    markers.forEach(marker=>{
      const raw=marker.getAttribute(selector);
      const index=raw==null?-1:Number(raw);
      const active=index===activeIndex&&activeIndex>=0;
      const under=index===contactIndex&&contactIndex>=0;
      marker.classList.toggle('active',active);
      marker.classList.toggle('under-playhead',under);
    });
  }
  function nearestKeyIndex(keys,property,t,tolerance){
    let best=-1,bestDistance=Infinity;
    for(let i=0;i<keys.length;i++){
      const key=keys[i];if(!key||(property&&key.property!==property))continue;
      const d=Math.abs(Number(key.t)-t);
      if(Number.isFinite(d)&&d<=tolerance&&d<bestDistance){best=i;bestDistance=d;}
    }
    return best;
  }
  function syncPlayheadKeySelection(){
    const id=(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null)?'':String(selectedClip);
    const t=timelineTime();if(!Number.isFinite(t))return;
    if(!id){
      const hadChar=typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'&&selectedKfIdx!==null;
      if(hadChar){selectedKfIdx=null;if(typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();}
      window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe=null;
      return;
    }
    const pps=typeof pxPerSecond==='function'?Math.max(1,Number(pxPerSecond())||1):100;
    const objectClip=typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectClipById(id):null;
    const isCharacter=(id==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(id));
    const isCharacterLayer=/^char\d+$/.test(id);
    if(objectClip){
      if(typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'&&selectedKfIdx!==null){selectedKfIdx=null;if(typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();}
      const property=typeof window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeProperty==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeProperty(objectClip):(objectClip.type==='audio'?'volume':'transform');
      const keys=Array.isArray(objectClip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)?objectClip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes:[];
      const tolerance=typeof window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeContactWindow==='function'?Number(window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeContactWindow()):Math.max(.05,9/pps);
      const idx=nearestKeyIndex(keys,property,t,Math.max(.001,tolerance));
      const chosen=window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe;
      if(idx>=0){
        if(!chosen||String(chosen.id)!==id||Number(chosen.index)!==idx)window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe={id,index:idx,source:'playhead'};
      }else if(chosen&&String(chosen.id)===id){window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe=null;}
      let track=window.__ethvelleActiveObjectKeyframeTrack;
      if(!track||!track.isConnected||String(track.dataset.objectKfTrack||'')!==id)track=document.querySelector(`[data-object-kf-track="${id.replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/"/g,'\\"')}"]`);
      if(track){window.__ethvelleActiveObjectKeyframeTrack=track;const selected=window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe;const active=selected&&String(selected.id)===id?Number(selected.index):-1;setMarkerState(track,'data-object-kf-idx',active,idx);}
      return;
    }
    if(isCharacterLayer){
      /* A character ROW selection still represents its active main animation clip. */
      window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe=null;
      const keys=activeClip&&Array.isArray(activeClip.keyframes)?activeClip.keyframes:[];
      const tolerance=typeof window.keyframeContactWindow==='function'?Number(window.keyframeContactWindow()):Math.max(.05,9/pps);
      const idx=nearestKeyIndex(keys,null,t,Math.max(.001,tolerance));
      const previous=typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'?selectedKfIdx:null;
      if(typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined')selectedKfIdx=idx>=0?idx:null;
      setMarkerState($('kfTrack'),'data-kf-idx',idx,idx);
      if((previous==null?-1:Number(previous))!==idx&&typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();
      return;
    }
    if(isCharacter){
      window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe=null;
      const clip=typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(id):null;
      const keys=clip&&Array.isArray(clip.keyframes)?clip.keyframes:[];
      const tolerance=typeof window.keyframeContactWindow==='function'?Number(window.keyframeContactWindow()):Math.max(.05,9/pps);
      const idx=nearestKeyIndex(keys,null,t,Math.max(.001,tolerance));
      const previous=typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'?selectedKfIdx:null;
      if(typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined')selectedKfIdx=idx>=0?idx:null;
      const track=$(id==='main'?'kfTrack':'kfTrack-'+id);
      setMarkerState(track,'data-kf-idx',idx,idx);
      if((previous==null?-1:Number(previous))!==idx&&typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();
      return;
    }
    const chosen=window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe;
    if(chosen&&String(chosen.id)!==id)window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe=null;
    if(typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'&&selectedKfIdx!==null){selectedKfIdx=null;if(typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();}
  }
  let keySyncRaf=0;
  function scheduleKeySync(){if(keySyncRaf)return;keySyncRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{keySyncRaf=0;try{syncPlayheadKeySelection();}catch(err){console.warn('Keyframe contact sync:',err);}});}
  const oldSelectForKeySync=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelectForKeySync==='function'&&!oldSelectForKeySync.__evv91KeySync){
    window.selectClipForChrome=function(){const result=oldSelectForKeySync.apply(this,arguments);scheduleKeySync();return result;};
    window.selectClipForChrome.__evv91KeySync=true;
  }
  const oldUpdateKey=window.updateKeyButtonState;
  if(typeof oldUpdateKey==='function'&&!oldUpdateKey.__evv91Contact){
    window.updateKeyButtonState=function(){const result=oldUpdateKey.apply(this,arguments);scheduleKeySync();return result;};
    window.updateKeyButtonState.__evv91Contact=true;
  }
  const oldSetTime=window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime;
  if(typeof oldSetTime==='function'&&!oldSetTime.__evv91Contact){
    window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime=function(){const result=oldSetTime.apply(this,arguments);scheduleKeySync();return result;};
    window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime.__evv91Contact=true;
  }
  const oldSeek=window.seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds;
  if(typeof oldSeek==='function'&&!oldSeek.__evv91Contact){
    window.seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds=function(){const result=oldSeek.apply(this,arguments);scheduleKeySync();return result;};
    window.seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds.__evv91Contact=true;
  }
  const oldMovePlayhead=window.movePlayhead;
  if(typeof oldMovePlayhead==='function'&&!oldMovePlayhead.__evv91Contact){
    window.movePlayhead=function(){const result=oldMovePlayhead.apply(this,arguments);scheduleKeySync();return result;};
    window.movePlayhead.__evv91Contact=true;
  }
  const oldRender=window.renderFrame;
  if(typeof oldRender==='function'&&!oldRender.__evv91Contact){
    window.renderFrame=function(){const result=oldRender.apply(this,arguments);scheduleKeySync();return result;};
    window.renderFrame.__evv91Contact=true;
  }

  /* A direct pointer selection is more reliable on Android than depending on the synthesized
     click after pointer capture / timeline scrolling. The click is suppressed only for that
     same marker so the old inline handler never selects it twice. */
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const marker=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('#timelineInner .kf-marker'):null;
    if(!marker||e.isPrimary===false)return;
    const objectTrack=marker.closest('.object-keyframe-track');
    const rawObject=marker.getAttribute('data-object-kf-idx');
    const rawChar=marker.getAttribute('data-kf-idx');
    marker.dataset.evV91PointerAt=String(Date.now());
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    try{
      if(objectTrack&&rawObject!=null){
        const id=String(objectTrack.dataset.objectKfTrack||'');
        if(id&&typeof window.selectObjectKeyframe==='function')window.selectObjectKeyframe(id,Number(rawObject));
      }else if(rawChar!=null){
        const clipEl=marker.closest('.anim-clip');
        const ref=clipEl&&clipEl.id==='animClipEl'?'main':clipEl&&clipEl.dataset.segIdx!=null?'seg'+clipEl.dataset.segIdx:'';
        if(ref&&typeof window.selectKeyframe==='function')window.selectKeyframe(Number(rawChar),ref);
      }
    }catch(err){console.warn('Keyframe pointer selection:',err);}
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    const marker=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('#timelineInner .kf-marker'):null;
    if(!marker)return;
    const stamp=Number(marker.dataset.evV91PointerAt)||0;
    if(stamp&&Date.now()-stamp<900){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();delete marker.dataset.evV91PointerAt;}
  },true);

  /* 4) Reverse is restricted to real video overlays. Backgrounds, images, text and other
     normal layers never receive the Reverse action. Adjustment sliders auto-apply their
     CSS filter live; the panel deliberately has no confirmation/check action. */
  function enforceVideoReverseVisibility(){
    const reverse=$('clipReverseTool');if(!reverse)return;
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'?'':(selectedClip||''));
    const clip=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===id):null;
    reverse.style.display=clip&&clip.type==='video'?'flex':'none';
  }
  const oldSyncTools=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSyncTools==='function'&&!oldSyncTools.__evv91VideoReverse){
    window.syncBottomToolsMode=function(){const result=oldSyncTools.apply(this,arguments);enforceVideoReverseVisibility();return result;};
    window.syncBottomToolsMode.__evv91VideoReverse=true;
  }
  const oldSelectClip=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelectClip==='function'&&!oldSelectClip.__evv91VideoReverse){
    window.selectClipForChrome=function(){const result=oldSelectClip.apply(this,arguments);enforceVideoReverseVisibility();return result;};
    window.selectClipForChrome.__evv91VideoReverse=true;
  }
  enforceVideoReverseVisibility();
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'&&$('bottomToolsClip'))new MutationObserver(enforceVideoReverseVisibility).observe($('bottomToolsClip'),{childList:true,subtree:true});
  document.addEventListener('change',e=>{if(e.target&&e.target.matches('#evAdj_brightness,#evAdj_contrast,#evAdj_saturation,#evAdj_hue,#evAdj_blur,#evAdj_grayscale,#evAdj_sepia')){clearTimeout(window.__ethvelleAdjustmentPersistTimer);if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}},true);
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v93-transform-compat-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  // Ensure legacy visual overlays resolve to their own transform data before the v84
  // Transform bridge reads the active target. Non-media/character behavior remains untouched.
  const priorTarget=window.transformTarget;
  if(typeof priorTarget==='function'&&!priorTarget.__evv93MediaTarget){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const id=(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null)?String(selectedClip):'';
      const bgList=typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[];
      const ovList=typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];
      if(id.startsWith('bg')){
        const bg=bgList.find(x=>String(x.id)===id);
        if(bg){if(!bg.transform)bg.transform={scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};return {obj:bg.transform,kind:'background',bgClip:bg};}
      }
      if(id){
        const ov=ovList.find(x=>String(x.id)===id);
        if(ov&&(ov.type==='image'||ov.type==='video')){
          if(!ov.transform)ov.transform={scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};
          return {obj:ov.transform,kind:'overlay',ov};
        }
      }
      return priorTarget.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evv93MediaTarget=true;window.transformTarget=wrapped;
  }
  // Ruler values are already changed by the v91 pointer handler before it dispatches input.
  // Re-sync the ruler's tick-strip offset after the selected media transform is applied.
  const priorUpdate=window.updateTransform;
  if(typeof priorUpdate==='function'&&!priorUpdate.__evv93Apply){
    const wrapped=function(){
      // The v84 media bridge already writes the selected clip's live/keyframed transform
      // and repaints that one media layer. Do not repaint it a second time here (which
      // becomes expensive while dragging the ruler on a phone).
      const result=priorUpdate.apply(this,arguments);
      try{window.ethvelleSyncTransformRulers?.();}catch(_e){}
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv93Apply=true;window.updateTransform=wrapped;
  }
  // Refresh the transform inputs after opening the panel (including a restored legacy clip).
  const priorOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof priorOpen==='function'&&!priorOpen.__evv93TransformOpen){
    const wrapped=function(key){const result=priorOpen.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='transform')requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{window.syncTransformSliders?.();window.ethvelleSyncTransformRulers?.();}catch(_e){}});return result;};
    wrapped.__evv93TransformOpen=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

    border-radius:50%!important;border:1px solid #454550!important;box-shadow:none!important;
  }

  /* Native momentum scrolling is much lighter than the old JS pointer-capture loop. */
  
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none!important;width:0!important;height:0!important;}
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip > .tool-item,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip .tool-item{
    flex:0 0 auto!important;flex-shrink:0!important;touch-action:pan-x!important;
  }
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v94-final-sync-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  // Re-render only the overlay list when the sheet is opened; video thumbnails are generated
  // lazily by renderOverlayListPanel() and the first stored frame is always used in its tile.
  const oldOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function'&&!oldOpen.__evv94OverlayThumbs){
    const wrapped=function(key){const result=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='overlay')requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{window.renderOverlayListPanel?.();}catch(_e){}});return result;};
    wrapped.__evv94OverlayThumbs=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>


<!-- Ethvelle v96: remove competing toolbar pointer-scroll driver; preserve ruler frame while shortening tick marks. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v95-crop-and-latency-css">
  /* The Transform panel stays the same size. Only the gray vertical ruler marks become half-height. */

    width:100%!important;max-width:100%!important;height:78px!important;
  }

  }

  }
  /* The requested Transform reset control is removed; Crop's separate Reset crop remains untouched. */
  #toolPanel .evtr-root > .evtr-footer{display:none!important;}

  /* A horizontal toolbar drag is handled immediately by touchmove below; leave vertical gestures to the page. */
  
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsDefault::-webkit-scrollbar,

  }

    pointer-events:auto!important;touch-action:none!important;z-index:30!important;

  }
  #canvasStage #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle:active{cursor:grabbing!important;}
  #canvasStage #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle::after{pointer-events:none!important;}

<script id="ethvelle-v96-direct-crop-and-performance-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(v)||0));

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
     1. Manual crop: use the actual transformed crop-root quad to map
        screen movement into the crop frame's local coordinate system.
        This includes canvas zoom, parent rotation and media rotation.
     ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  let cropDrag=null;
  function getCropContext(){
    const id=String(window.__ethvelleCropEditingId||((typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null)?selectedClip:''));
    if(!id)return null;
    let clip=null;
    try{clip=typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectClipById(id):null;}catch(_e){}
    if(!clip)return null;
    const ovs=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:[];
    const bgs=(typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds))?backgrounds:[];
    let el=null,kind='';
    if(ovs.includes(clip)){
      kind='overlay';
      el=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[id]:null;
      if(!el){const node=document.querySelector(`.overlay-media-item[data-overlay-id="${id.replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/"/g,'\\"')}"]`);el=node?.querySelector('img,video,canvas')||node;}
    }else if(bgs.includes(clip)){
      kind='background';el=$('bgImgLayer');
    }
    if(!kind)return null;
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage)return null;
    const sw=Math.max(1,stage.clientWidth),sh=Math.max(1,stage.clientHeight);
    const iw=Math.max(1,el&&(el.videoWidth||el.naturalWidth||el.width)||Number(clip.sourceWidth)||sw);
    const ih=Math.max(1,el&&(el.videoHeight||el.naturalHeight||el.height)||Number(clip.sourceHeight)||sh);
    const scale=Math.min(sw/iw,sh/ih),w=iw*scale,h=ih*scale;
    return {id,clip,kind,el,stage,sw,sh,iw,ih,m:{x:(sw-w)/2,y:(sh-h)/2,w,h,aspect:iw/ih}};
  }
  function cropValues(clip){
    const raw=typeof window.ethvelleCropFractions==='function'?window.ethvelleCropFractions(clip.crop):(clip.crop||{});
    return {left:clamp(raw.left,0,.92),right:clamp(raw.right,0,.92),top:clamp(raw.top,0,.92),bottom:clamp(raw.bottom,0,.92)};
  }
  function normalizeCrop(c){
    let left=clamp(c.left,0,.92),right=clamp(c.right,0,.92),top=clamp(c.top,0,.92),bottom=clamp(c.bottom,0,.92);
    if(left+right>.92){const f=.92/(left+right);left*=f;right*=f;}
    if(top+bottom>.92){const f=.92/(top+bottom);top*=f;bottom*=f;}
    return {left,right,top,bottom};
  }
  function localToClientInverse(root,stage){
    // Preferred path: getBoxQuads includes every transform in the ancestor chain and the
    // cropRoot's own translate/rotate/scale. Its basis vectors map local pixels to screen pixels.
    try{
      if(typeof root.getBoxQuads==='function'){
        const q=root.getBoxQuads({box:'border'})[0]||root.getBoxQuads()[0];
        const w=Math.max(1,root.offsetWidth),h=Math.max(1,root.offsetHeight);
        if(q&&q.p1&&q.p2&&q.p4){
          const a=(q.p2.x-q.p1.x)/w,b=(q.p2.y-q.p1.y)/w;
          const c=(q.p4.x-q.p1.x)/h,d=(q.p4.y-q.p1.y)/h;
          const det=a*d-b*c;
          if(Math.abs(det)>.000001)return {a:d/det,b:-b/det,c:-c/det,d:a/det};
        }
      }
    }catch(_e){}
    // Fallback: compose the 2D transforms of the crop root and every ancestor. Translation
    // and transform-origin do not affect deltas; the linear matrix does. This also handles
    // inline-canvas zoom/rotation in browsers where getBoxQuads is unavailable.
    try{
      const M=window.DOMMatrixReadOnly||window.DOMMatrix;
      let a=1,b=0,c=0,d=1;
      for(let node=root;node&&node.nodeType===1;node=node.parentElement){
        const cs=getComputedStyle(node),raw=cs.transform;
        let na=1,nb=0,nc=0,nd=1;
        if(M&&raw&&raw!=='none'){
          const matrix=new M(raw);na=matrix.a;nb=matrix.b;nc=matrix.c;nd=matrix.d;
        }
        const zoom=parseFloat(cs.zoom);const z=Number.isFinite(zoom)&&zoom>0?zoom:1;
        na*=z;nb*=z;nc*=z;nd*=z;
        const nextA=na*a+nc*b,nextB=nb*a+nd*b,nextC=na*c+nc*d,nextD=nb*c+nd*d;
        a=nextA;b=nextB;c=nextC;d=nextD;
      }
      const det=a*d-b*c;
      if(Math.abs(det)>.000001)return {a:d/det,b:-b/det,c:-c/det,d:a/det};
      return {a:1,b:0,c:0,d:1};
    }catch(_e){return {a:1,b:0,c:0,d:1};}
  }
  function paintManualCrop(ctx,c){
    const root=$('evV89CropRoot'),frame=$('evV89CropFrame');
    if(!root||!frame)return;
    const m=ctx.m;
    frame.style.left=(m.x+c.left*m.w)+'px';
    frame.style.top=(m.y+c.top*m.h)+'px';
    // The visible frame must match the exact crop fractions. A fixed 18px minimum
    // made very small selections look larger than the area that Apply/export actually used.
    frame.style.width=Math.max(1,m.w*(1-c.left-c.right))+'px';
    frame.style.height=Math.max(1,m.h*(1-c.top-c.bottom))+'px';
  }
  function cropPointerInStage(e,root,stage,inverse){
    // Convert the contact point to the crop-root's local stage coordinates. getBoxQuads
    // gives the true transformed top-left; the centre-based fallback remains correct for
    // the root's 50% transform-origin when getBoxQuads is unavailable in a WebView.
    try{
      if(typeof root.getBoxQuads==='function'){
        const q=root.getBoxQuads({box:'border'})[0]||root.getBoxQuads()[0];
        if(q&&q.p1){
          const dx=e.clientX-q.p1.x,dy=e.clientY-q.p1.y;
          return {x:inverse.a*dx+inverse.c*dy,y:inverse.b*dx+inverse.d*dy};
        }
      }
    }catch(_e){}
    const r=root.getBoundingClientRect();
    const dx=e.clientX-(r.left+r.width/2),dy=e.clientY-(r.top+r.height/2);
    return {x:stage.clientWidth/2+inverse.a*dx+inverse.c*dy,
      y:stage.clientHeight/2+inverse.b*dx+inverse.d*dy};
  }
  function startManualCrop(e){
    const root=$('evV89CropRoot'),frame=$('evV89CropFrame');
    if(!root||!frame||getComputedStyle(root).display==='none')return false;
    if(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0)return false;
    const ctx=getCropContext();if(!ctx)return false;
    // While Crop is open the full-stage overlay owns the gesture. Android WebView can
    // report the overlay/root instead of a tiny transformed handle as event.target, so
    // never require closest('#evV89CropFrame') to succeed before hit-testing the frame.
    const stageRect=ctx.stage.getBoundingClientRect();
    const onStage=e.clientX>=stageRect.left&&e.clientX<=stageRect.right&&
      e.clientY>=stageRect.top&&e.clientY<=stageRect.bottom;
    if(!onStage&&!root.contains(e.target))return false;
    const inverse=localToClientInverse(root,ctx.stage);
    const p=cropPointerInStage(e,root,ctx.stage,inverse);
    const left=parseFloat(frame.style.left)||0,top=parseFloat(frame.style.top)||0;
    const width=parseFloat(frame.style.width)||frame.offsetWidth||18;
    const height=parseFloat(frame.style.height)||frame.offsetHeight||18;
    const right=left+width,bottom=top+height;
    const explicitHandle=e.target?.closest?.('.ev-v89-crop-handle')?.dataset.handle||'';
    let handle=explicitHandle;
    // Use the eight visible grip centres as forgiving 28px touch targets. This also
    // works when the browser dispatches the touch to the frame/root rather than the grip.
    if(!handle){
      const centres={nw:[left,top],n:[left+width/2,top],ne:[right,top],e:[right,top+height/2],
        se:[right,bottom],s:[left+width/2,bottom],sw:[left,bottom],w:[left,top+height/2]};
      let nearest='',nearestD=Infinity;
      Object.entries(centres).forEach(([name,point])=>{
        const dist=Math.hypot(p.x-point[0],p.y-point[1]);
        if(dist<nearestD){nearestD=dist;nearest=name;}
      });
      if(nearestD<=29)handle=nearest;
    }
    // The whole perimeter is also draggable, not just the eight grip squares.
    if(!handle){
      const edgeBand=20;
      const verticalSpan=p.y>=top-edgeBand&&p.y<=bottom+edgeBand;
      const horizontalSpan=p.x>=left-edgeBand&&p.x<=right+edgeBand;
      const nearL=verticalSpan&&Math.abs(p.x-left)<=edgeBand;
      const nearR=verticalSpan&&Math.abs(p.x-right)<=edgeBand;
      const nearT=horizontalSpan&&Math.abs(p.y-top)<=edgeBand;
      const nearB=horizontalSpan&&Math.abs(p.y-bottom)<=edgeBand;
      if(nearT&&nearL)handle='nw';
      else if(nearT&&nearR)handle='ne';
      else if(nearB&&nearL)handle='sw';
      else if(nearB&&nearR)handle='se';
      else if(nearL)handle='w';
      else if(nearR)handle='e';
      else if(nearT)handle='n';
      else if(nearB)handle='s';
    }
    const inside=p.x>=left&&p.x<=right&&p.y>=top&&p.y<=bottom;
    if(!handle&&!inside){
      // Do not let a tap outside the crop frame select or move the media underneath.
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      return true;
    }
    if(!handle)handle='move';
    cropDrag={pointerId:e.pointerId,ctx,handle,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,
      startCrop:cropValues(ctx.clip),inverse,root,frame,capture:root,
      ratio:document.querySelector('.ev-v89-ratio.active')?.dataset.ratio||'free'};
    // Capture on the full-stage overlay: its geometry does not change as the frame is
    // resized, so pointer capture remains stable throughout the gesture.
    try{root.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    return true;
  }
  function updateManualCrop(e){
    const d=cropDrag;if(!d||d.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return false;
    if(!d.frame.isConnected||!d.root.isConnected){cropDrag=null;return false;}
    const ctx=d.ctx,inv=d.inverse;
    const sx=e.clientX-d.startX,sy=e.clientY-d.startY;
    // Convert the finger's screen-space delta directly into stage/crop-root local pixels.
    const lx=inv.a*sx+inv.c*sy,ly=inv.b*sx+inv.d*sy;
    const dx=lx/Math.max(1,ctx.m.w),dy=ly/Math.max(1,ctx.m.h);
    const c={...d.startCrop},h=d.handle;
    const w0=1-d.startCrop.left-d.startCrop.right,hh0=1-d.startCrop.top-d.startCrop.bottom;
    const min=.04;
    if(h==='move'){
      c.left=clamp(d.startCrop.left+dx,0,1-w0);c.right=1-w0-c.left;
      c.top=clamp(d.startCrop.top+dy,0,1-hh0);c.bottom=1-hh0-c.top;
    }else if(d.ratio==='free'){
      if(h.includes('w'))c.left=clamp(d.startCrop.left+dx,0,1-d.startCrop.right-min);
      if(h.includes('e'))c.right=clamp(d.startCrop.right-dx,0,1-d.startCrop.left-min);
      if(h.includes('n'))c.top=clamp(d.startCrop.top+dy,0,1-d.startCrop.bottom-min);
      if(h.includes('s'))c.bottom=clamp(d.startCrop.bottom-dy,0,1-d.startCrop.top-min);
    }else{
      const ratioParts=String(d.ratio).split(':').map(Number);
      const wanted=(ratioParts[0]/ratioParts[1])/ctx.m.aspect;
      if(!Number.isFinite(wanted)||wanted<=0){cropDrag=null;return false;}
      if(h==='e'||h==='w'){
        let w=clamp(w0+(h==='e'?dx:-dx),min,1),height=clamp(w/wanted,min,1);w=height*wanted;
        if(h==='e')c.right=1-d.startCrop.left-w;else c.left=1-d.startCrop.right-w;
        c.top=(1-height)/2;c.bottom=1-height-c.top;
      }else if(h==='n'||h==='s'){
        let height=clamp(hh0+(h==='s'?dy:-dy),min,1),w=clamp(height*wanted,min,1);height=w/wanted;
        if(h==='s')c.bottom=1-d.startCrop.top-height;else c.top=1-d.startCrop.bottom-height;
        c.left=(1-w)/2;c.right=1-w-c.left;
      }else{
        const dirX=h.includes('w')?-1:1,dirY=h.includes('n')?-1:1;
        const wCandidate=clamp(w0+dirX*dx,min,1),hCandidate=clamp(hh0+dirY*dy,min,1);
        let w=Math.abs(wCandidate-w0)/Math.max(min,w0)>=Math.abs(hCandidate-hh0)/Math.max(min,hh0)?wCandidate:hCandidate*wanted;
        let height=w/wanted;
        if(height>1){height=1;w=height*wanted;}if(w>1){w=1;height=w/wanted;}
        if(dirX<0)c.left=1-d.startCrop.right-w;else c.right=1-d.startCrop.left-w;
        if(dirY<0)c.top=1-d.startCrop.bottom-height;else c.bottom=1-d.startCrop.top-height;
      }
    }
    const next=normalizeCrop(c);
    ctx.clip.crop=next;
    paintManualCrop(ctx,next);
    // Update readouts only; do not rebuild the media renderer or persist on every pointer frame.
    ['left','top','right','bottom'].forEach(k=>{const v=$('evcr-'+k+'-value');if(v)v.textContent=Math.round(next[k]*100)+'%';const input=$('evcr-'+k);if(input)input.value=Math.round(next[k]*100);});
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    return true;
  }
  function finishManualCrop(e){
    if(!cropDrag||e.pointerId!==undefined&&e.pointerId!==cropDrag.pointerId)return false;
    const d=cropDrag;cropDrag=null;
    try{if(e.pointerId!==undefined&&d.capture?.hasPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId))d.capture.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(_e){}
    e?.preventDefault?.();e?.stopPropagation?.();if(e?.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    return true;
  }
  // Window capture runs before the old document-level crop handler and before canvas drag handlers.
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(startManualCrop(e))return;},{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(cropDrag)updateManualCrop(e);},{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{if(cropDrag)finishManualCrop(e);},{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>{if(cropDrag)finishManualCrop(e);},{capture:true,passive:false});

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
     2. Native bottom tool-strip movement. The final CSS enables native
        horizontal overflow scrolling and Android momentum without competing
        JS touch/pointer scroll drivers.
     ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /*
     The bottom tool rows now use native horizontal overflow scrolling. This avoids
     competing touch/pointer scroll drivers and lets Android provide its own smooth
     compositor scrolling and momentum. Tool taps remain native/clickable.
  */

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------
     3. Coalesce selection-box rendering to one paint per display frame.
        Drag values still update on every input event; only DOM painting
        is coalesced. Flush before pointer-up saves the final transform.
     ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  const baseSelBoxApply=window.selBoxApply;
  let pendingSelTarget=null,selPaintRaf=0,selPaintThis=null,lastDragSliderSync=0;
  const syncTransformBase=window.syncTransformSliders;
  if(typeof syncTransformBase==='function'&&!syncTransformBase.__evv95DragThrottle){
    const throttled=function(){
      if(window.__ethvelleLayerDragPainting){
        const now=performance.now();if(now-lastDragSliderSync<90)return;
        lastDragSliderSync=now;
      }
      return syncTransformBase.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    throttled.__evv95DragThrottle=true;window.syncTransformSliders=throttled;
  }
  function flushSelPaint(){
    if(selPaintRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(selPaintRaf);selPaintRaf=0;}
    if(!pendingSelTarget||typeof baseSelBoxApply!=='function')return;
    const target=pendingSelTarget,context=selPaintThis;pendingSelTarget=null;selPaintThis=null;
    window.__ethvelleLayerDragPainting=true;
    try{baseSelBoxApply.call(context,target);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle drag paint:',err);}
    finally{window.__ethvelleLayerDragPainting=false;}
  }
  if(typeof baseSelBoxApply==='function'){
    const coalesced=function(tg){pendingSelTarget=tg;selPaintThis=this;if(!selPaintRaf)selPaintRaf=requestAnimationFrame(flushSelPaint);};
    coalesced.__evv95Raf=true;window.selBoxApply=coalesced;
    document.addEventListener('pointerup',flushSelPaint,true);
    document.addEventListener('pointercancel',flushSelPaint,true);
  }

  /* Transform sliders previously called the full media renderer on every input event.
     Keep the keyframe/model writes from the existing updateTransform(), but draw only the
     selected media element during that short slider gesture. */
  const baseRenderOverlay=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof baseRenderOverlay==='function'&&!baseRenderOverlay.__evv95FastTransform){
    const fastRender=function(time){
      if(window.__ethvelleFastTransformInput){
        try{
          const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');
          const clip=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===id):null;
          if(clip&&(clip.type==='image'||clip.type==='video')&&typeof window.applyOverlayTransformPreviewFast==='function'){
            window.applyOverlayTransformPreviewFast({obj:clip.transform||{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0}});
            if(typeof window.updateSelBox==='function')window.updateSelBox();
            return;
          }
        }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle fast transform render:',err);}
      }
      return baseRenderOverlay.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    fastRender.__evv95FastTransform=true;window.renderOverlayMedia=fastRender;
  }
  const baseUpdateTransform=window.updateTransform;
  if(typeof baseUpdateTransform==='function'&&!baseUpdateTransform.__evv95FastTransform){
    const fastUpdate=function(){
      window.__ethvelleFastTransformInput=true;
      try{return baseUpdateTransform.apply(this,arguments);}
      finally{window.__ethvelleFastTransformInput=false;}
    };
    fastUpdate.__evv95FastTransform=true;window.updateTransform=fastUpdate;
  }

  /* Character-joint drawing is also painted at most once per display frame while a pointer
     is held on the canvas. Pose calculations themselves remain immediate. */
  const baseDrawStick=window.drawStick;
  if(typeof baseDrawStick==='function'&&!baseDrawStick.__evv95Raf){
    let canvasPointerActive=false,pendingDraw=false,drawRaf=0,drawingNow=false,drawArgs=[];
    function flushDraw(){
      if(drawRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(drawRaf);drawRaf=0;}
      if(!pendingDraw)return;
      pendingDraw=false;drawingNow=true;
      try{baseDrawStick.apply(window,drawArgs);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle canvas drag paint:',err);}
      finally{drawingNow=false;drawArgs=[];}
    }
    const wrappedDraw=function(){
      if((!canvasPointerActive&&!window.__ethvelleFastTransformInput)||drawingNow)return baseDrawStick.apply(this,arguments);
      pendingDraw=true;drawArgs=Array.from(arguments);
      if(!drawRaf)drawRaf=requestAnimationFrame(flushDraw);
    };
    wrappedDraw.__evv95Raf=true;window.drawStick=wrappedDraw;
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(e.target?.closest?.('#stickCanvas'))canvasPointerActive=true;},{capture:true,passive:true});
    const endCanvas=e=>{if(!canvasPointerActive)return;canvasPointerActive=false;flushDraw();};
    document.addEventListener('pointerup',endCanvas,true);
    document.addEventListener('pointercancel',endCanvas,true);
    window.addEventListener('blur',()=>{canvasPointerActive=false;flushDraw();});
  }
})();
</script>

<!-- Ethvelle v97: committed crop persistence, screenshot-style Transform sliders,
     native bottom-toolbar scrolling, and compositor-based layer dragging. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v97-performance-and-transform-css">
  /* Match the supplied Transform reference: one compact panel with four full-width sliders. */
  #toolPanel .evv-transform-slider-panel{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:0;padding:4px 0 8px;}
  #toolPanel .evv-transform-slider-panel .transform-group{margin-bottom:25px;}

<script id="ethvelle-v97-crop-commit-guard-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  // The crop fractions are already part of each image/video/background clip and are
  // consumed by preview + export. Re-apply the crop transform after renderer passes so
  // a later base-transform paint cannot visually undo an applied crop.
  function applyCommittedCrop(){
    try{
      if(window.__ethvelleCropEditingId)return;
      if(typeof window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview==='function')window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview();
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle crop commit refresh:',err);}
  }
  function wrapCropRender(name){
    const base=window[name];
    if(typeof base!=='function'||base.__evv97CropCommit)return;
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=base.apply(this,arguments);
      applyCommittedCrop();
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv97CropCommit=true;
    window[name]=wrapped;
  }
  // This final wrapper is deliberately installed after all earlier preview optimizers.
  wrapCropRender('renderOverlayMedia');
  wrapCropRender('applyBackground');
  wrapCropRender('applyBgTransform');

  // Apply crop after the Crop panel closes, then reassert it once more on the following
  // frame. This covers Android WebView's delayed image/video style updates and guarantees
  // that pressing Apply commits the same crop the export path already reads from clip.crop.
  const baseClose=window.closePanel;
  if(typeof baseClose==='function'&&!baseClose.__evv97CropCommit){
    const wrappedClose=function(){
      const cropId=String(window.__ethvelleCropEditingId||'');
      const result=baseClose.apply(this,arguments);
      if(cropId){
        const commit=()=>{
          try{
            const clip=typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectClipById(cropId):null;
            if(clip&&typeof window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview==='function')window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview();
          }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle crop apply:',err);}
        };
        requestAnimationFrame(()=>{commit();requestAnimationFrame(commit);});
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedClose.__evv97CropCommit=true;
    window.closePanel=wrappedClose;
  }

  // No MutationObserver is attached to the media layer: observing every video-frame
  // style update would add unnecessary work during playback and timeline interactions.
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v98-direct-interactions-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  /* ----- Transform sheet fit ----- */
  function fitTransformPanelV98(){
    const editor=$('screen-editor'),panel=$('toolPanel');
    const transport=editor&&editor.querySelector('.transport-row');
    if(!editor||!panel||!transport||!panel.classList.contains('open'))return;
    const er=editor.getBoundingClientRect(),rr=transport.getBoundingClientRect();
    const vv=window.visualViewport;
    const visibleBottom=vv?Math.min(er.bottom,vv.offsetTop+vv.height):Math.min(er.bottom,window.innerHeight||er.bottom);
    const bottomGap=8;
    const available=Math.max(150,Math.floor(visibleBottom-bottomGap-(rr.bottom+5)));
    panel.classList.add('ev-v89-fit-panel');
    panel.style.setProperty('--ev-v98-panel-max-height',available+'px');
    const header=panel.querySelector('.tool-panel-header');
    const headerHeight=header?header.getBoundingClientRect().height:48;
    const body=$('panelBody');
    if(body)body.style.setProperty('--ev-v98-panel-body-max-height',Math.max(90,available-headerHeight-2)+'px');
  }
  const baseOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof baseOpen==='function'&&!baseOpen.__evv98Fit){
    const wrapped=function(key){
      const result=baseOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='transform')requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(fitTransformPanelV98));
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv98Fit=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
  const resizeFit=()=>{if($('toolPanel')?.classList.contains('ev-v89-fit-panel'))requestAnimationFrame(fitTransformPanelV98);};
  window.addEventListener('resize',resizeFit,{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',resizeFit,{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport)window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',resizeFit,{passive:true});

  /* Bottom toolbars use the browser's native horizontal scroller. A custom pointer-capture
     loop here competed with Android's own touch/momentum pipeline and caused stutter. */

  /* ----- Crop and selection frame use identical source geometry ----- */
  function cropFractions(clip){
    let c={left:0,right:0,top:0,bottom:0};
    try{c=typeof window.ethvelleCropFractions==='function'?window.ethvelleCropFractions(clip.crop):(clip.crop||c);}catch(_e){}
    const clamp=v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(.92,Number(v)||0));
    let left=clamp(c.left),right=clamp(c.right),top=clamp(c.top),bottom=clamp(c.bottom);
    if(left+right>.92){const f=.92/(left+right);left*=f;right*=f;}
    if(top+bottom>.92){const f=.92/(top+bottom);top*=f;bottom*=f;}
    return {left,right,top,bottom};
  }
  function sourceGeometry(clip,element,kind,target){
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage||!clip||!element)return;
    const sw=Math.max(1,stage.clientWidth||window.stageW||1),sh=Math.max(1,stage.clientHeight||window.stageH||1);
    const iw=Math.max(1,Number(element.videoWidth||element.naturalWidth||clip.sourceWidth)||sw);
    const ih=Math.max(1,Number(element.videoHeight||element.naturalHeight||clip.sourceHeight)||sh);
    const base=Math.min(sw/iw,sh/ih),crop=cropFractions(clip);
    const cropW=Math.max(1,iw*(1-crop.left-crop.right));
    const cropH=Math.max(1,ih*(1-crop.top-crop.bottom));
    const hasCrop=crop.left+crop.right+crop.top+crop.bottom>.0001;
    const desired=hasCrop?Math.max(sw/cropW,sh/cropH):base;
    const factor=desired/Math.max(.0001,base);
    const dx=hasCrop?(crop.right-crop.left)*iw*desired/2:0;
    const dy=hasCrop?(crop.bottom-crop.top)*ih*desired/2:0;
    target.w0=iw*base*factor;
    target.h0=ih*base*factor;
    const baseTransform=target.obj||clip.transform||{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};
    const now=typeof window.getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?window.getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec):0);
    const tracked=kind==='overlay'&&typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform==='function'
      ?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(clip,now):baseTransform;
    const trackedOn=!!(kind==='overlay'&&clip&&clip.clipTracking&&clip.clipTracking.enabled&&clip.clipTracking.targetId);
    const objectKeyframe=kind==='overlay'&&typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'
      ?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(clip,'transform',now):null;
    // Match the renderer's active transform source. During a cropped video preview the
    // committed crop renderer uses the tracked transform; images continue to use their
    // object keyframe when one exists. Without crop, use the regular renderer's keyframe
    // fallback to the tracked/base transform.
    const keyed=kind==='overlay'
      ?((trackedOn||(hasCrop&&clip.type==='video'))?tracked:(objectKeyframe||tracked))
      :baseTransform;
    target.obj={...keyed};
    if(hasCrop){
      const motion=kind==='overlay'&&typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function'
        ?window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(clip,now):{x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1};
      target.obj.offsetX=(Number(keyed.offsetX)||0)+dx+(Number(motion.x)||0);
      target.obj.offsetY=(Number(keyed.offsetY)||0)+dy+(Number(motion.y)||0);
      target.obj.rotation=(Number(keyed.rotation)||0)+(Number(motion.rotation)||0);
      target.obj.scale=(Number(keyed.scale)||1)*(Number(motion.scale)||1);
    }
  }
  const baseTarget=window.selBoxTarget;
  if(typeof baseTarget==='function'&&!baseTarget.__evv98CropBounds){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const target=baseTarget.apply(this,arguments);if(!target)return target;
      try{
        const now=typeof window.getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?window.getCurrentAbsoluteTime():Number(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
        if(target.kind==='overlay'){
          const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:(window.selectedClip??''));
          const list=typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:(Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[]);
          const clip=list.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===id&&['image','video'].includes(o.type));
          const mediaMap=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls:window.overlayMediaEls;
          const el=clip&&mediaMap?mediaMap[id]:null;
          if(clip&&el)sourceGeometry(clip,el,'overlay',target);
        }else if(target.kind==='background'){
          const clip=typeof backgroundClipForSelection==='function'?backgroundClipForSelection():null;
          const el=$('bgImgLayer');
          if(clip&&el)sourceGeometry(clip,el,'background',target);
        }
      }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle crop selection geometry:',err);}
      return target;
    };
    wrapped.__evv98CropBounds=true;window.selBoxTarget=wrapped;
  }
  if(typeof window.updateSelBox==='function'){
    const oldUpdate=window.updateSelBox;
    if(!oldUpdate.__evv98CropRefresh){
      const wrapped=function(){const result=oldUpdate.apply(this,arguments);return result;};
      wrapped.__evv98CropRefresh=true;window.updateSelBox=wrapped;
    }
  }
})();
</script>

    color:#fff!important;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(95,139,255,.18)!important;
  }
  #toolPanel .ev-v89-crop-actions{gap:10px!important;padding-top:12px!important;border-top:1px solid #30384A!important;}
  #toolPanel .ev-v89-crop-actions button{min-height:44px!important;border-radius:11px!important;font-size:12.5px!important;}

  #toolPanel .ev-v89-crop-done{flex:1 1 auto!important;background:linear-gradient(165deg,#4E7CFF,#1E46C4)!important;color:#fff!important;box-shadow:0 5px 14px rgba(47,92,232,.2)!important;}

  /* Help & Support hierarchy: blue section/field labels, white FAQ questions and contact
     values, muted-gray FAQ explanations, and brand-coloured contact marks. */
  #screen-help .settings-group-label{color:#78A0FF!important;font-weight:800!important;letter-spacing:.045em!important;}
  #screen-help .settings-card{background:linear-gradient(155deg,#1B1F2B,#17171C)!important;border:1px solid #2D3548!important;}

    min-height:68px!important;gap:14px!important;padding:15px 16px!important;margin-bottom:10px!important;

  /* Timeline/toolbar surfaces stay on their own scrolling layers to reduce paint work. */

    scroll-behavior:auto!important;overscroll-behavior:contain!important;

  }
  #screen-editor #timelineScroll{will-change:scroll-position!important;}

<script id="ethvelle-v99-interaction-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const raf=()=>new Promise(resolve=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>resolve()));

  /* The earlier fit patch measured max-height but left the sheet bottom-anchored to an
     editor box that can extend below Android's visible viewport. Use a top+bottom constrained
     absolute sheet instead: both edges are guaranteed to remain within the visible viewport. */
  function fitTransformPanelV99(){
    const editor=$('screen-editor'),panel=$('toolPanel'),transport=editor?.querySelector('.transport-row');
    if(!editor||!panel||!transport||!panel.classList.contains('open'))return;
    const er=editor.getBoundingClientRect(),rr=transport.getBoundingClientRect(),vv=window.visualViewport;
    const visibleTop=Math.max(er.top,vv?vv.offsetTop:0);
    const visibleBottom=Math.min(er.bottom,vv?(vv.offsetTop+vv.height):document.documentElement.clientHeight);
    let topViewport=Math.max(visibleTop+8,rr.bottom+6);
    const bottomGap=Math.max(8,er.bottom-visibleBottom+8);
    if(visibleBottom-topViewport-bottomGap<164)topViewport=Math.max(visibleTop+8,visibleBottom-bottomGap-164);
    const available=Math.max(150,Math.floor(visibleBottom-topViewport-bottomGap));
    panel.classList.add('evv99-panel-fit','ev-v89-fit-panel');
    const header=panel.querySelector('.tool-panel-header'),body=$('panelBody');
    const naturalHeight=(header?header.getBoundingClientRect().height:48)+(body?body.scrollHeight:300)+2;
    const fittedHeight=Math.min(available,Math.max(160,naturalHeight));
    panel.style.setProperty('--evv99-panel-top',Math.max(0,topViewport-er.top)+'px');
    panel.style.setProperty('--evv99-panel-height',fittedHeight+'px');
    panel.style.setProperty('--evv99-panel-max-height',available+'px');
    if(body){body.style.removeProperty('max-height');body.style.removeProperty('height');}
  }
  function clearTransformPanelFit(){
    const panel=$('toolPanel');if(!panel)return;
    panel.classList.remove('evv99-panel-fit');
    panel.style.removeProperty('--evv99-panel-top');panel.style.removeProperty('--evv99-panel-height');panel.style.removeProperty('--evv99-panel-max-height');
  }
  const originalOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof originalOpen==='function'&&!originalOpen.__evv99Fit){
    const wrapped=function(key){
      const result=originalOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='transform')requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(fitTransformPanelV99));
      else clearTransformPanelFit();
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv99Fit=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
  const resizeFit=()=>{if($('toolPanel')?.classList.contains('open')&&$('toolPanel')?.classList.contains('evv99-panel-fit'))requestAnimationFrame(fitTransformPanelV99);};
  window.addEventListener('resize',resizeFit,{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',resizeFit,{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport){
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',resizeFit,{passive:true});
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll',resizeFit,{passive:true});
  }
  const transport= $('screen-editor')?.querySelector('.transport-row');
  if(transport&&typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){const ro=new ResizeObserver(resizeFit);ro.observe(transport);}

  /* Only move a layer icon when the white left trim tab actually overlaps its normal icon
     rectangle. Reconcile only the affected row, and only write row classes when their state
     really changes. The old MutationObserver was passed its records array directly to an
     event-style handler; records have no .target, so every mutation forced a full-row scan. */
  const timeline=$('timelineInner');
  let badgeRaf=0,badgeNeedsFullScan=false;
  const pendingBadgeRows=new Set();
  const badgeOwnedClasses=new Set(['evv99-icon-shift','evv104-bg-selected']);
  function setBadgeRowClass(row,name,enabled){
    if(!row||!row.classList)return;
    const has=row.classList.contains(name),want=!!enabled;
    if(has!==want)row.classList.toggle(name,want);
  }
  function updateLayerBadgeClearance(){
    badgeRaf=0;if(!timeline)return;
    const rows=badgeNeedsFullScan?Array.from(timeline.querySelectorAll('.layer-row')):Array.from(pendingBadgeRows);
    pendingBadgeRows.clear();badgeNeedsFullScan=false;
    rows.forEach(row=>{
      if(!row||!row.isConnected)return;
      // Background keeps the same left-clearance movement as the other clip rows even
      // when its selected clip has been moved away from timeline time zero.
      const bgSelected=!!row.querySelector('#track-background .track-clip.selected');
      setBadgeRowClass(row,'evv104-bg-selected',bgSelected);
      const badge=row.querySelector('.layer-badge');
      const clip=row.querySelector('.anim-clip.selected,.track-clip.selected,.overlay-clip.selected');
      if(!badge||!clip){setBadgeRowClass(row,'evv99-icon-shift',false);return;}
      const track=clip.closest('.layer-track');if(!track){setBadgeRowClass(row,'evv99-icon-shift',false);return;}
      const b=badge.getBoundingClientRect(),c=clip.getBoundingClientRect();
      const wasShifted=row.classList.contains('evv99-icon-shift');
      const badgeLeft=b.left+(wasShifted?21:0),badgeRight=b.right+(wasShifted?21:0);
      const tabLeft=c.left-21,tabRight=c.left-1;
      const overlap=Math.min(tabRight,badgeRight)-Math.max(tabLeft,badgeLeft);
      setBadgeRowClass(row,'evv99-icon-shift',overlap>2);
    });
  }
  function queueBadgeClearance(node,fullScan=false){
    const element=node&&node.nodeType===1?node:null;
    if(fullScan||!element){badgeNeedsFullScan=true;}
    else{
      const row=element.closest&&element.closest('.layer-row');
      if(row)pendingBadgeRows.add(row);
      else badgeNeedsFullScan=true;
    }
    if(!badgeRaf)badgeRaf=requestAnimationFrame(updateLayerBadgeClearance);
  }
  function scheduleBadgeClearance(event){
    queueBadgeClearance(event&&event.target?event.target:null);
  }
  function classChangeIsOnlyBadgeState(record){
    if(!record||record.attributeName!=='class'||record.oldValue==null)return false;
    const before=new Set(String(record.oldValue||'').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean));
    const after=new Set(String(record.target&&record.target.getAttribute('class')||'').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean));
    const changed=new Set([...before,...after]);
    for(const token of changed){if(before.has(token)!==after.has(token)&&!badgeOwnedClasses.has(token))return false;}
    return true;
  }
  function nodeContainsLayerRowOrClip(node){
    if(!node||node.nodeType!==1)return false;
    if(node.matches&&node.matches('.layer-row,.anim-clip,.track-clip,.overlay-clip'))return true;
    return !!(node.querySelector&&node.querySelector('.layer-row,.anim-clip,.track-clip,.overlay-clip'));
  }
  function handleTimelineMutations(records){
    let fullScan=false,hasQueuedRow=false;
    for(const record of records){
      if(record.type==='attributes'&&record.attributeName==='class'){
        // Our own clearance marker changes are final output, not new layout inputs. Also
        // ignore unrelated class changes; only selection changes on a timeline clip matter.
        if(classChangeIsOnlyBadgeState(record))continue;
        const target=record.target;
        if(!target||!target.matches||!target.matches('.anim-clip,.track-clip,.overlay-clip'))continue;
        const before=new Set(String(record.oldValue||'').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean));
        const after=target.classList;
        if(before.has('selected')===after.contains('selected'))continue;
        const row=target.closest('.layer-row');
        if(row){pendingBadgeRows.add(row);hasQueuedRow=true;}
        continue;
      }
      if(record.type==='childList'){
        const target=record.target;
        const row=target&&target.nodeType===1&&target.closest?target.closest('.layer-row'):null;
        const hasRelevantNodes=[...(record.addedNodes||[]),...(record.removedNodes||[])].some(nodeContainsLayerRowOrClip);
        if(!hasRelevantNodes)continue;
        if(row){pendingBadgeRows.add(row);hasQueuedRow=true;}
        else fullScan=true;
      }
    }
    if(fullScan)queueBadgeClearance(null,true);
    else if(hasQueuedRow&&!badgeRaf)badgeRaf=requestAnimationFrame(updateLayerBadgeClearance);
  }
  if(timeline){
    queueBadgeClearance(null,true);
    const observer=new MutationObserver(handleTimelineMutations);
    observer.observe(timeline,{subtree:true,childList:true,attributes:true,attributeOldValue:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
    // Layer badges and their clips share the same horizontal scroll transform, so scrolling
    // cannot change their overlap. During pointer interaction only the touched row is checked.
    timeline.addEventListener('pointerdown',scheduleBadgeClearance,{passive:true});
    timeline.addEventListener('pointerup',scheduleBadgeClearance,{passive:true});
    timeline.addEventListener('pointercancel',scheduleBadgeClearance,{passive:true});
    window.addEventListener('resize',()=>queueBadgeClearance(null,true),{passive:true});
    // Selection-class mutations above already enqueue the old and new clip rows. Do not
    // wrap every selection in a full timeline scan: that forced geometry reads for every
    // layer (and a synchronous layout) even when only one row changed.
  }

  /* Professional crop surface: replace the ratio control body while continuing to use
     the established normalized crop model and the working pointer/preview/export pipeline. */
  if(typeof panelContent!=='undefined'){
    panelContent.crop={title:'Crop',body:`<div class="ev-v89-crop-panel evv99-crop-panel">
      <div class="ev-v89-ratio-label">ASPECT RATIO</div>
      <div class="ev-v99-crop-hint">Drag inside the frame to reposition. Drag a corner or edge to refine the crop.</div>
      <div class="ev-v89-ratios" role="group" aria-label="Crop aspect ratio">
        <button type="button" class="ev-v89-ratio active" data-ratio="free" aria-pressed="true" onclick="ethvelleV89SetCropRatio('free')">Free</button>
        <button type="button" class="ev-v89-ratio" data-ratio="1:1" aria-pressed="false" onclick="ethvelleV89SetCropRatio('1:1')">1:1</button>
        <button type="button" class="ev-v89-ratio" data-ratio="16:9" aria-pressed="false" onclick="ethvelleV89SetCropRatio('16:9')">16:9</button>
        <button type="button" class="ev-v89-ratio" data-ratio="9:16" aria-pressed="false" onclick="ethvelleV89SetCropRatio('9:16')">9:16</button>
        <button type="button" class="ev-v89-ratio" data-ratio="4:3" aria-pressed="false" onclick="ethvelleV89SetCropRatio('4:3')">4:3</button>
        <button type="button" class="ev-v89-ratio" data-ratio="3:4" aria-pressed="false" onclick="ethvelleV89SetCropRatio('3:4')">3:4</button>
      </div>
      <div class="ev-v89-crop-actions"><button type="button" class="ev-v89-crop-reset" onclick="ethvelleV89ResetCrop()">Reset</button><button type="button" class="ev-v89-crop-done" onclick="closePanel()">Apply crop</button></div>
    </div>`};
  }
  // Keep ratio state visibly and accessibly synchronized after choosing a preset.
  const originalSetRatio=window.ethvelleV89SetCropRatio;
  if(typeof originalSetRatio==='function'&&!originalSetRatio.__evv99Ratio){
    const wrapped=function(value){const result=originalSetRatio.apply(this,arguments);document.querySelectorAll('.ev-v89-ratio').forEach(b=>{const active=b.dataset.ratio===String(value||'free');b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',active?'true':'false');});return result;};
    wrapped.__evv99Ratio=true;window.ethvelleV89SetCropRatio=wrapped;
  }
  const originalResetCrop=window.ethvelleV89ResetCrop;
  if(typeof originalResetCrop==='function'&&!originalResetCrop.__evv99Reset){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=originalResetCrop.apply(this,arguments);document.querySelectorAll('.ev-v89-ratio').forEach(b=>{const active=b.dataset.ratio==='free';b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',active?'true':'false');});return result;};
    wrapped.__evv99Reset=true;window.ethvelleV89ResetCrop=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

<!-- Ethvelle v100: high-refresh timeline interaction, persistent no-zoom crop, and panel fit. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v100-performance-crop-css">
  /* The Transform sheet begins at the transport row's top edge, covering Play/Pause while
     leaving the canvas above it unobstructed. It fills the remaining visible phone viewport. */
  #screen-editor #toolPanel.tool-panel.evv99-panel-fit.evv100-panel-fit{
    top:var(--evv99-panel-top,220px)!important;
    bottom:auto!important;
    height:var(--evv99-panel-height,420px)!important;
    max-height:var(--evv99-panel-max-height,420px)!important;
    min-height:180px!important;
    display:flex!important;flex-direction:column!important;overflow:hidden!important;
    box-sizing:border-box!important;contain:layout paint!important;
  }
  #screen-editor #toolPanel.evv100-panel-fit .tool-panel-header{flex:0 0 auto!important;}
  #screen-editor #toolPanel.evv100-panel-fit .tool-panel-body{
    flex:1 1 0%!important;min-height:0!important;height:auto!important;max-height:none!important;
    overflow-x:hidden!important;overflow-y:auto!important;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch!important;
    overscroll-behavior:contain!important;touch-action:pan-y!important;
    padding-bottom:calc(14px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px))!important;
  }
  /* The crop panel is intentionally icon/control-only; remove the explanatory sentence. */
  #toolPanel .ev-v99-crop-hint{display:none!important;}
  /* Help headings and contact field names use the same deep, solid primary blue as the app. */
  #screen-help .settings-group-label,
  #screen-help .help-contact-card .hc-text b{color:#2F5CE8!important;text-shadow:none!important;}
  /* Keep answers readable without turning FAQ questions or contact values blue. */
  #screen-help .help-a{color:#AEB6C7!important;}
  /* Dedicated compositor surfaces: avoid smooth-scroll interpolation and repeated paints. */
  
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsDefault > .tool-item,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip > .tool-item,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe > .tool-item,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsDefault button,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip button,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe button{
    touch-action:pan-x pan-y!important;flex:0 0 auto!important;
  }
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsDefault::-webkit-scrollbar,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip::-webkit-scrollbar,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none!important;width:0!important;height:0!important;}
  #screen-editor #timelineScroll{scroll-behavior:auto!important;will-change:scroll-position!important;}

<script id="ethvelle-v100-performance-crop-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  /* Expand Transform upward to the Play/Pause row, not upward into the canvas. */
  function fitTransformPanelV100(){
    const editor=$('screen-editor'),panel=$('toolPanel'),transport=editor?.querySelector('.transport-row');
    if(!editor||!panel||!transport||!panel.classList.contains('open'))return;
    const er=editor.getBoundingClientRect(),rr=transport.getBoundingClientRect(),vv=window.visualViewport;
    const visibleTop=Math.max(er.top,vv?vv.offsetTop:0);
    const visibleBottom=Math.min(er.bottom,vv?(vv.offsetTop+vv.height):(window.innerHeight||er.bottom));
    const bottomGap=Math.max(8,er.bottom-visibleBottom+8);
    const stageRect=editor.querySelector('#canvasStage')?.getBoundingClientRect();
    const maxTop=Math.max(visibleTop+3,rr.top-2);
    const desiredTop=Math.max(visibleTop+3,rr.top-20);
    // The canvas bottom is a hard boundary. Use the gap above transport when available,
    // but never pull the panel upward through the stage on short screens. If the viewport
    // leaves only a shallow panel area, the panel body scrolls internally instead.
    const safeTop=Math.min(maxTop,Math.max(visibleTop+3,stageRect?stageRect.bottom+4:rr.top-2));
    const topViewport=Math.min(maxTop,Math.max(safeTop,desiredTop));
    const availableHeight=Math.max(0,Math.floor(visibleBottom-bottomGap-topViewport));
    const height=Math.max(0,availableHeight);
    panel.classList.add('evv99-panel-fit','evv100-panel-fit');
    panel.style.setProperty('--evv99-panel-top',Math.max(0,topViewport-er.top)+'px');
    panel.style.setProperty('--evv99-panel-height',height+'px');
    panel.style.setProperty('--evv99-panel-max-height',height+'px');
    const body=$('panelBody');if(body){body.style.removeProperty('max-height');body.style.removeProperty('height');}
  }
  function clearTransformPanelV100(){
    const panel=$('toolPanel');if(!panel)return;
    panel.classList.remove('evv100-panel-fit');
  }
  const oldOpenPanel=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpenPanel==='function'&&!oldOpenPanel.__evv100Panel){
    const wrapped=function(key){
      const result=oldOpenPanel.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='transform')requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(fitTransformPanelV100));
      else clearTransformPanelV100();
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv100Panel=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
  const onResize=()=>{if($('toolPanel')?.classList.contains('evv100-panel-fit'))requestAnimationFrame(fitTransformPanelV100);};
  window.addEventListener('resize',onResize,{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',onResize,{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport){window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',onResize,{passive:true});window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll',onResize,{passive:true});}

  /*
   * Non-zoom crop renderer.
   * Crop fractions clip pixels inside the media's existing object-fit:contain bounds.
   * The media transform (scale/position/rotation) is not changed by crop, so it cannot
   * jump larger after Apply. The clip-path is reasserted after each normal renderer pass,
   * preventing later preview ticks from silently restoring the uncropped media.
   */
  function cropFor(clip){
    let c={left:0,right:0,top:0,bottom:0};
    try{c=window.ethvelleCropFractions?window.ethvelleCropFractions(clip?.crop):(clip?.crop||c);}catch(_e){}
    const clamp=v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(.92,Number(v)||0));
    let left=clamp(c.left),right=clamp(c.right),top=clamp(c.top),bottom=clamp(c.bottom);
    if(left+right>.92){const f=.92/(left+right);left*=f;right*=f;}
    if(top+bottom>.92){const f=.92/(top+bottom);top*=f;bottom*=f;}
    return {left,right,top,bottom};
  }
  const cropPathCache=new WeakMap();
  function applyCropClipPath(el,clip,stage,source){
    if(!el||!el.style)return;
    const c=cropFor(clip),active=c.left+c.right+c.top+c.bottom>.0001;
    if(!active){
      if(el.style.clipPath!=='none')el.style.clipPath='none';
      if(el.style.webkitClipPath!=='none')el.style.webkitClipPath='none';
      cropPathCache.delete(el);return;
    }
    const sw=Math.max(1,stage.clientWidth||stageW||1),sh=Math.max(1,stage.clientHeight||stageH||1);
    const iw=Math.max(1,Number(source?.videoWidth||source?.naturalWidth||clip.sourceWidth)||sw);
    const ih=Math.max(1,Number(source?.videoHeight||source?.naturalHeight||clip.sourceHeight)||sh);
    const signature=[sw,sh,iw,ih,c.left,c.right,c.top,c.bottom].join('|');
    const cached=cropPathCache.get(el);
    if(cached&&cached.signature===signature){
      if(el.style.clipPath!==cached.value)el.style.clipPath=cached.value;
      if(el.style.webkitClipPath!==cached.value)el.style.webkitClipPath=cached.value;
      return;
    }
    const fit=Math.min(sw/iw,sh/ih),contentW=iw*fit,contentH=ih*fit;
    const letterX=(sw-contentW)/2,letterY=(sh-contentH)/2;
    const top=letterY+c.top*contentH;
    const right=letterX+c.right*contentW;
    const bottom=letterY+c.bottom*contentH;
    const left=letterX+c.left*contentW;
    const value=`inset(${top.toFixed(3)}px ${right.toFixed(3)}px ${bottom.toFixed(3)}px ${left.toFixed(3)}px)`;
    cropPathCache.set(el,{signature,value});
    if(el.style.clipPath!==value)el.style.clipPath=value;
    if(el.style.webkitClipPath!==value)el.style.webkitClipPath=value;
  }
  function applyCropWithoutZoom(){
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage)return;
    const list=typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];
    const map=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls:{};
    const editingId=window.__ethvelleCropEditingId==null?'':String(window.__ethvelleCropEditingId);
    for(let i=0;i<list.length;i++){
      const ov=list[i];if(!ov||(ov.type!=='image'&&ov.type!=='video'))continue;
      const id=String(ov.id),el=map[id];if(!el)continue;
      if(id===editingId){
        // While the crop frame is open, keep the original source fully visible for editing.
        if(el.style.clipPath!=='none')el.style.clipPath='none';
        if(el.style.webkitClipPath!=='none')el.style.webkitClipPath='none';
        cropPathCache.delete(el);
      }else applyCropClipPath(el,ov,stage,el);
      const chroma=typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'&&overlayChromaCanvases?overlayChromaCanvases[id]:null;
      if(chroma){
        if(id===editingId){chroma.style.clipPath='none';chroma.style.webkitClipPath='none';cropPathCache.delete(chroma);}
        else applyCropClipPath(chroma,ov,stage,el);
      }
    }
    const now=typeof window.getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?Number(window.getCurrentAbsoluteTime()):(Number(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0);
    const bg=typeof window.currentBackgroundClip==='function'?window.currentBackgroundClip(now):null;
    const img=$('bgImgLayer');
    if(img){
      if(bg)applyCropClipPath(img,bg,stage,img);
      else if(img.style.clipPath!=='none'){
        img.style.clipPath='none';img.style.webkitClipPath='none';cropPathCache.delete(img);
      }
    }
  }
  window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview=applyCropWithoutZoom;
  function wrapCropRender(name){
    const base=window[name];if(typeof base!=='function'||base.__evv100NoZoomCrop)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const result=base.apply(this,arguments);applyCropWithoutZoom();return result;};
    wrapped.__evv100NoZoomCrop=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  }
  wrapCropRender('renderOverlayMedia');
  wrapCropRender('applyBackground');
  wrapCropRender('applyBgTransform');
  // Also refresh after a complete render so a background/overlay selected during playback
  // receives the same saved crop even when another plugin wrapper changes its style.
  const baseRenderFrame=window.renderFrame;
  if(typeof baseRenderFrame==='function'&&!baseRenderFrame.__evv100NoZoomCrop){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=baseRenderFrame.apply(this,arguments);applyCropWithoutZoom();return result;};
    wrapped.__evv100NoZoomCrop=true;window.renderFrame=wrapped;
  }

  /*
   * Match the selection-box geometry to the original unzoomed source bounds.
   * The frame continues to follow the layer transform/keyframes; crop does not inflate it.
   */
  const baseSelTarget=window.selBoxTarget;
  if(typeof baseSelTarget==='function'&&!baseSelTarget.__evv100NoZoomCrop){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const target=baseSelTarget.apply(this,arguments);if(!target)return target;
      try{
        const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage)return target;
        const now=typeof window.getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?Number(window.getCurrentAbsoluteTime()):(Number(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0);
        let clip=null,el=null,kind=target.kind;
        if(kind==='overlay'){
          const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:(window.selectedClip??''));
          const list=typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];
          clip=list.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===id&&(o.type==='image'||o.type==='video'))||null;
          el=clip&&typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'?overlayMediaEls[clip.id]:null;
        }else if(kind==='background'){
          clip=typeof backgroundClipForSelection==='function'?backgroundClipForSelection():null;
          el=$('bgImgLayer');
        }
        if(clip&&el){
          const sw=Math.max(1,stage.clientWidth||stageW||1),sh=Math.max(1,stage.clientHeight||stageH||1);
          const iw=Math.max(1,Number(el.videoWidth||el.naturalWidth||clip.sourceWidth)||sw),ih=Math.max(1,Number(el.videoHeight||el.naturalHeight||clip.sourceHeight)||sh);
          const contain=Math.min(sw/iw,sh/ih);
          const fullW=iw*contain,fullH=ih*contain;
          // Shrink the selection box to the cropped region so the white border hugs the visible content.
          let cL=0,cR=0,cT=0,cB2=0;
          try{
            const cropRaw=typeof window.ethvelleCropFractions==='function'?window.ethvelleCropFractions(clip.crop):(clip.crop||{});
            const clampC=v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(.92,Number(v)||0));
            cL=clampC(cropRaw.left);cR=clampC(cropRaw.right);cT=clampC(cropRaw.top);cB2=clampC(cropRaw.bottom);
            if(cL+cR>.92){const f=.92/(cL+cR);cL*=f;cR*=f;}
            if(cT+cB2>.92){const f=.92/(cT+cB2);cT*=f;cB2*=f;}
          }catch(_e){}
          const hasCrop=cL+cR+cT+cB2>.0001;
          if(hasCrop){
            // Cropped visible size
            target.w0=fullW*(1-cL-cR);
            target.h0=fullH*(1-cT-cB2);
            // Shift the centre (cB) to the centre of the cropped region
            const letterX=(sw-fullW)/2,letterY=(sh-fullH)/2;
            const cropCx=letterX+(cL+1-cR)*fullW/2-(sw/2);
            const cropCy=letterY+(cT+1-cB2)*fullH/2-(sh/2);
            target.cB={x:sw/2+cropCx,y:sh/2+cropCy};
          }else{
            target.w0=fullW;target.h0=fullH;
          }
          const tracked=kind==='overlay'&&typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform==='function'?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(clip,now):(clip.transform||{});
          const trackedOn=!!(kind==='overlay'&&clip.clipTracking&&clip.clipTracking.enabled&&clip.clipTracking.targetId);
          const keyed=trackedOn?(tracked||clip.transform||{}):kind==='overlay'&&typeof window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeProperty==='function'&&window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeProperty(clip)==='transform'&&typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'
            ?(window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(clip,'transform',now)||tracked||clip.transform||{}):
            (kind==='background'&&typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?(window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(clip,'transform',now)||clip.transform||{}):(tracked||clip.transform||{}));
          const motion=kind==='overlay'&&typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function'?window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(clip,now):{x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1};
          target.obj={...(keyed||{}),offsetX:(Number(keyed?.offsetX)||0)+(Number(motion?.x)||0),offsetY:(Number(keyed?.offsetY)||0)+(Number(motion?.y)||0),rotation:(Number(keyed?.rotation)||0)+(Number(motion?.rotation)||0),scale:(Number(keyed?.scale)||1)*(Number(motion?.scale)||1)};
        }
      }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle v100 selection bounds:',err);}
      return target;
    };
    wrapped.__evv100NoZoomCrop=true;window.selBoxTarget=wrapped;
  }

})();
</script>
