<script id="ethvelle-v102-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  /* ── Patch: ensure updateSelBox is called after applyCropPreview so the
        selection border reflects the cropped size on every render tick. ── */
  const origApplyCropPreview=window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview;
  if(typeof origApplyCropPreview==='function'&&!origApplyCropPreview.__evv102Sel){
    window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview=function(){
      origApplyCropPreview.apply(this,arguments);
      // Re-anchor selection box to cropped bounds after clip-path is applied.
      if(typeof window.updateSelBox==='function')window.updateSelBox();
    };
    window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview.__evv102Sel=true;
  }

  /* ── Patch: fix FAQ arrows  -  force deep-blue stroke on every toggle ── */
  const origToggleHelpItem=window.toggleHelpItem;
  if(typeof origToggleHelpItem==='function'&&!origToggleHelpItem.__evv102Faq){
    window.toggleHelpItem=function(i){
      origToggleHelpItem.apply(this,arguments);
      // Re-paint arrow stroke after DOM toggle so CSS cascade wins cleanly.
      const el=document.getElementById('helpItem-'+i);
      if(el){const svg=el.querySelector('.help-q svg');if(svg)svg.style.stroke='#2F5CE8';}
    };
    window.toggleHelpItem.__evv102Faq=true;
  }

  /* ── Patch: limit how often updateSelBox fires during high-frequency pointer
        moves to reduce layout thrash on low-end Android devices. ── */
  const origUpdateSelBox=window.updateSelBox;
  if(typeof origUpdateSelBox==='function'&&!origUpdateSelBox.__evv102Throttle){
    let _selBoxPending=false;
    const _throttledSelBox=function(){
      if(_selBoxPending)return;_selBoxPending=true;
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>{_selBoxPending=false;origUpdateSelBox.apply(window,arguments);});
    };
    // Only throttle the standalone updateSelBox; selBoxApply still calls the original directly
    // (via its own rAF chain) so drag responsiveness isn't hurt.
    _throttledSelBox.__evv102Throttle=true;
    // Wrap only the external callers (RAF-loop, resize events).
    const _origDrawStick=window.drawStick;
    if(typeof _origDrawStick==='function'&&!_origDrawStick.__evv102SelThrottle){
      // drawStick already calls updateSelBox inside; wrap to use throttled version.
      // Actually don't override drawStick  -  too risky. Just ensure crop preview stays fast.
    }
  }

  /* ── Patch: invalidate cropPathCache on renderFrame when crop editing is active
        so Apply always produces a fresh clip-path, not a stale cached one. ── */
  const _origRenderFrame=window.renderFrame;
  if(typeof _origRenderFrame==='function'&&!_origRenderFrame.__evv102CropInvalidate){
    window.renderFrame=function(){
      const result=_origRenderFrame.apply(this,arguments);
      // After render, ensure crop is re-applied (idempotent if already applied).
      if(typeof window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview==='function')window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview();
      return result;
    };
    window.renderFrame.__evv102CropInvalidate=true;
  }

})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v104-media-timeline-extent-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /*
   * Every row lives in the same timelineInner coordinate space. Some legacy add paths
   * rebuild the Clips/Text/Audio rows after updating timelineDuration but omit the width
   * sync. Reconcile only when the content width is actually short; this does not rebuild
   * media rows and does not run on every animation frame.
   */
  const baseRender=window.renderOverlayLayers;
  if(typeof baseRender==='function'&&!baseRender.__evv104TimelineExtent){
    let extentRaf=0;
    const syncIfShort=()=>{
      extentRaf=0;
      try{
        if(typeof window.ensureTimelineCoversContent==='function')window.ensureTimelineCoversContent();
        const inner=document.getElementById('timelineInner');
        const scroller=document.getElementById('timelineScroll');
        if(!inner||!scroller||typeof window.syncTimelineInnerWidth!=='function')return;
        const pps=typeof window.pxPerSecond==='function'?Number(window.pxPerSecond()):0;
        const duration=typeof timelineDuration!=='undefined'?Number(timelineDuration):0;
        if(!(pps>0&&duration>0))return;
        const pad=scroller.clientWidth/2;
        const wanted=duration*pps+pad*2;
        const current=parseFloat(inner.style.width)||inner.getBoundingClientRect().width||0;
        if(wanted>current+1)window.syncTimelineInnerWidth();
      }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle timeline extent sync:',err);}
    };
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=baseRender.apply(this,arguments);
      if(!extentRaf)extentRaf=requestAnimationFrame(syncIfShort);
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv104TimelineExtent=true;
    window.renderOverlayLayers=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v105-smart-loading-performance-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  // v88 limited crop margins to 45% per side (and 80% per axis). The newer manual crop
  // handles and selection geometry support 92%, so the old limit made Apply/preview stop
  // shrinking even though the blue frame could be dragged farther. Keep every crop reader
  // on the same normalized range so small Free and preset crops are applied exactly.
  window.ethvelleCropFractions=function(value){
    const source=value&&typeof value==='object'?value:{};
    const clamp=v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(.92,Number(v)||0));
    let left=clamp(source.left),right=clamp(source.right),top=clamp(source.top),bottom=clamp(source.bottom);
    if(left+right>.92){const f=.92/(left+right);left*=f;right*=f;}
    if(top+bottom>.92){const f=.92/(top+bottom);top*=f;bottom*=f;}
    return {left,right,top,bottom};
  };

  // Compact temporary toast: shown only after a clip's long-press timer has entered move mode.
  window.ethvelleNotifyClipMoveReady=function(){
    try{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Ready to move clip  -  drag to reposition',{duration:1250});}catch(_e){}
  };
  window.ethvelleNotifyClipMoved=function(deltaSeconds){
    const delta=Number(deltaSeconds);if(!Number.isFinite(delta)||Math.abs(delta)<0.0001)return;
    try{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(delta>0?'Clip moved forward':'Clip moved backward',{duration:1150});}catch(_e){}
  };

  // One reusable full-screen loader is used only for the text composer and character uploader.
  let globalLoader=null;
  function getGlobalLoader(){
    if(globalLoader&&globalLoader.isConnected)return globalLoader;
    globalLoader=$('evv105GlobalLoader');
    if(!globalLoader){
      globalLoader=document.createElement('div');globalLoader.id='evv105GlobalLoader';
      globalLoader.className='evv105-global-loader';globalLoader.setAttribute('role','status');
      globalLoader.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');
      globalLoader.innerHTML='<div class="evv105-global-loader-card"><div class="evv105-loader"><span class="evv105-spinner" aria-hidden="true"></span><b class="evv105-loader-title">Loading…</b><small>Preparing editing tools</small></div></div>';
      document.body.appendChild(globalLoader);
    }
    return globalLoader;
  }
  function showGlobalLoader(label){
    const node=getGlobalLoader();const title=node.querySelector('.evv105-loader-title');
    if(title)title.textContent='Loading '+String(label||'tools')+'…';
    node.classList.add('active');
  }
  function hideGlobalLoader(){const node=$('evv105GlobalLoader');if(node)node.classList.remove('active');}
  function panelLoaderMarkup(label){
    return '<div class="evv105-loader" role="status" aria-live="polite"><span class="evv105-spinner" aria-hidden="true"></span><b>Loading '+String(label||'tools')+'…</b><small>Preparing this section</small></div>';
  }

  // Heavy panel UI is initialized only after the user opens that tool. Basic editing tools
  // (Select, Split, Crop, Keyframe, Transform, Undo/Redo) keep their immediate path.
  const heavyPanelKeys=new Set([
    'assets','anim','overlay','audio','audioAdjust','audioEffects','audioEnhance','audioNoise','audioVoice','audioPitch',
    'chroma','evltEnhance','evltAdjustments','clipAnimations','clipFilter','characterTracking'
  ]);
  const panelTitles={
    assets:'Assets',anim:'Character Animation',overlay:'Media Library',audio:'Audio Tools',audioAdjust:'Audio Adjustments',
    audioEffects:'Audio Effects',audioEnhance:'Audio Enhance',audioNoise:'Background Noise',audioVoice:'Voice Filters',audioPitch:'Pitch',
    chroma:'Chroma Key',evltEnhance:'Enhance',evltAdjustments:'Adjustments',clipAnimations:'Clip Animations',
    clipFilter:'Filters',characterTracking:'Character Tracking'
  };
  let panelLoadToken=0,activeLazyPanel='';
  const previousOpenPanel=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof previousOpenPanel==='function'&&!previousOpenPanel.__evv105Lazy){
    const lazyOpenPanel=function(key){
      const k=String(key||'');
      if(!heavyPanelKeys.has(k)){
        panelLoadToken++;activeLazyPanel='';
        return previousOpenPanel.apply(this,arguments);
      }
      // Keep the original no-character guard and all of the original click behavior.
      if(k==='anim'&&typeof characters!=='undefined'&&!characters.length){
        panelLoadToken++;activeLazyPanel='';return previousOpenPanel.apply(this,arguments);
      }
      if(typeof panelContent==='undefined'||!panelContent[k]){
        panelLoadToken++;activeLazyPanel='';return previousOpenPanel.apply(this,arguments);
      }
      const token=++panelLoadToken,args=Array.from(arguments),ctx=this;
      activeLazyPanel=k;
      const panel=$('toolPanel'),body=$('panelBody'),backdrop=$('panelBackdrop'),title=$('panelTitle');
      if(!panel||!body||!backdrop)return previousOpenPanel.apply(this,args);
      document.querySelectorAll('.tool-item').forEach(t=>t.classList.remove('active'));
      const tool=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.tool-item')).find(t=>t.dataset.tool===k);
      if(tool)tool.classList.add('active');
      if(title)title.textContent=(panelContent[k]&&panelContent[k].title)||panelTitles[k]||'Tools';
      body.dataset.evv105LastKey=k;body.setAttribute('aria-busy','true');body.innerHTML=panelLoaderMarkup(panelTitles[k]||panelContent[k].title);
      backdrop.classList.add('active');panel.classList.add('open');
      // A short visible loading state, then the existing renderer builds the real controls.
      setTimeout(()=>{
        requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
          if(token!==panelLoadToken||activeLazyPanel!==k||!panel.classList.contains('open')||body.dataset.evv105LastKey!==k)return;
          try{previousOpenPanel.apply(ctx,args);}
          finally{
            if(token===panelLoadToken&&body.dataset.evv105LastKey===k){body.removeAttribute('aria-busy');}
          }
        });
      },115);
      return undefined;
    };
    lazyOpenPanel.__evv105Lazy=true;window.openPanel=lazyOpenPanel;
  }

  // Closing a heavy panel cancels pending initialization and releases its generated controls.
  const previousClosePanel=window.closePanel;
  if(typeof previousClosePanel==='function'&&!previousClosePanel.__evv105Cleanup){
    const lazyClosePanel=function(){
      const key=activeLazyPanel||$('panelBody')?.dataset?.evv105LastKey||'';
      const token=++panelLoadToken;activeLazyPanel='';
      if(key==='assets'){
        try{assetRenderToken++;fontVisibilityObserver?.disconnect();fontVisibilityObserver=null;}catch(_e){}
      }
      const result=previousClosePanel.apply(this,arguments);
      if(heavyPanelKeys.has(key))requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
        const panel=$('toolPanel'),body=$('panelBody');
        if(token===panelLoadToken&&panel&&!panel.classList.contains('open')&&body){
          body.replaceChildren();body.removeAttribute('aria-busy');delete body.dataset.evv105LastKey;
        }
      });
      return result;
    };
    lazyClosePanel.__evv105Cleanup=true;window.closePanel=lazyClosePanel;
  }

  // Text tools use a separate full-screen composer, not #toolPanel. Defer construction until
  // the user explicitly opens it, then retain the existing draft/clip state on close.
  const previousTextOpen=window.openEthvelleTextComposer;
  let textOpenToken=0;
  if(typeof previousTextOpen==='function'&&!previousTextOpen.__evv105Lazy){
    const lazyTextOpen=function(){
      if($('ethvelleTextComposer')?.classList.contains('active'))return previousTextOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      const token=++textOpenToken,args=Array.from(arguments),ctx=this;
      showGlobalLoader('Text Editor');
      setTimeout(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
        if(token!==textOpenToken)return;
        hideGlobalLoader();previousTextOpen.apply(ctx,args);
      }),105);
      return undefined;
    };
    lazyTextOpen.__evv105Lazy=true;window.openEthvelleTextComposer=lazyTextOpen;
  }
  const previousTextClose=window.closeEthvelleTextComposer;
  if(typeof previousTextClose==='function'&&!previousTextClose.__evv105Cleanup){
    const closeText=function(){textOpenToken++;hideGlobalLoader();return previousTextClose.apply(this,arguments);};
    closeText.__evv105Cleanup=true;window.closeEthvelleTextComposer=closeText;
  }

  // Character rig/uploader tools are also deferred; no file picker is triggered from the timer.
  const previousOpenRig=window.openAutoRigUploader;
  let rigToken=0;
  if(typeof previousOpenRig==='function'&&!previousOpenRig.__evv105Lazy){
    const lazyOpenRig=function(){
      const token=++rigToken,args=Array.from(arguments),ctx=this;showGlobalLoader('Character Tools');
      setTimeout(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(token!==rigToken)return;hideGlobalLoader();previousOpenRig.apply(ctx,args);}),110);
      return undefined;
    };
    lazyOpenRig.__evv105Lazy=true;window.openAutoRigUploader=lazyOpenRig;
  }
  const previousCloseRig=window.closeAutoRigUploader;
  if(typeof previousCloseRig==='function'&&!previousCloseRig.__evv105Cleanup){
    const closeRig=function(){rigToken++;hideGlobalLoader();return previousCloseRig.apply(this,arguments);};
    closeRig.__evv105Cleanup=true;window.closeAutoRigUploader=closeRig;
  }

  // Model library: show an initial loader, then construct its rows in small batches.
  const previousOpenModelLibrary=window.openModelLibrary;
  const previousCloseModelLibrary=window.closeModelLibrary;
  let modelOpenToken=0,modelRenderToken=0;
  if(typeof previousOpenModelLibrary==='function'&&!previousOpenModelLibrary.__evv105Lazy){
    const lazyOpenModelLibrary=function(){
      const token=++modelOpenToken,backdrop=$('modelLibraryBackdrop'),list=$('modelAssetListBody');
      if(!backdrop||!list)return previousOpenModelLibrary.apply(this,arguments);
      backdrop.classList.add('active');list.innerHTML=panelLoaderMarkup('Character Assets');
      setTimeout(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
        if(token!==modelOpenToken||!backdrop.classList.contains('active'))return;
        previousOpenModelLibrary.apply(this,arguments);
      }),120);
      return undefined;
    };
    lazyOpenModelLibrary.__evv105Lazy=true;window.openModelLibrary=lazyOpenModelLibrary;
  }
  if(typeof previousCloseModelLibrary==='function'&&!previousCloseModelLibrary.__evv105Cleanup){
    const closeModelLibrary=function(){
      modelOpenToken++;modelRenderToken++;const result=previousCloseModelLibrary.apply(this,arguments);
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const b=$('modelLibraryBackdrop'),list=$('modelAssetListBody');if(b&&!b.classList.contains('active')&&list)list.replaceChildren();});
      return result;
    };
    closeModelLibrary.__evv105Cleanup=true;window.closeModelLibrary=closeModelLibrary;
  }

  // Build model-library tiles 12 at a time; keep all existing actions and project data intact.
  window.renderModelLibraryList=function(){
    const container=$('modelAssetListBody');if(!container)return;
    const token=++modelRenderToken;
    const ordered=typeof getAvailableModelAssets==='function'?getAvailableModelAssets():((Array.isArray(modelAssets)?modelAssets:[]).slice());
    container.replaceChildren();
    if(!ordered.length){const empty=document.createElement('p');empty.className='model-asset-empty';empty.textContent='No saved models yet  -  upload one to get started.';container.appendChild(empty);return;}
    const editSvg='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 20h9M16.5 3.5a2.12 2.12 0 013 3L7 19l-4 1 1-4L16.5 3.5z"/></svg>';
    const deleteSvg='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M3 6h18M8 6V4a2 2 0 012-2h4a2 2 0 012 2v2m3 0v14a2 2 0 01-2 2H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V6h14z"/></svg>';
    let index=0;
    const appendBatch=()=>{
      if(token!==modelRenderToken||!container.isConnected)return;
      const fragment=document.createDocumentFragment(),end=Math.min(index+12,ordered.length);
      for(;index<end;index++){
        const asset=ordered[index],id=String(asset.id||''),item=document.createElement('div');item.className='model-asset-item';
        const thumb=document.createElement('img');thumb.className='model-asset-thumb';thumb.alt='';thumb.loading='lazy';thumb.decoding='async';thumb.draggable=false;
        const fallbackSrc=typeof modelAssetThumbSrc==='function'?modelAssetThumbSrc(asset):'';
        const src=typeof modelGridThumbSrc==='function'?modelGridThumbSrc(asset):fallbackSrc;
        if(src)thumb.src=src;
        else{
          if(fallbackSrc)thumb.src=fallbackSrc;
          if(asset&&typeof modelFaceSourceFor==='function'&&modelFaceSourceFor(asset)&&typeof generateModelGridFullBodyPreview==='function')generateModelGridFullBodyPreview(asset).then(preview=>{if(preview&&thumb.isConnected)thumb.src=preview;}).catch(()=>{});
        }
        if(asset&&asset.origin==='admin'&&!(asset.sheet&&asset.sheet.parts)&&typeof evFaceTileFor==='function')evFaceTileFor(asset).then(preview=>{if(preview&&thumb.isConnected)thumb.src=preview;}).catch(()=>{});
        thumb.addEventListener('click',()=>window.useModelAsset(id));
        const name=document.createElement('span');name.className='model-asset-name';name.textContent=String(asset.name||'Untitled model');name.addEventListener('click',()=>window.useModelAsset(id));
        const actions=document.createElement('div');actions.className='model-asset-actions';
        const makeAction=(title,svg,fn,danger)=>{const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='model-asset-icon-btn'+(danger?' danger':'');el.title=title;el.setAttribute('role','button');el.setAttribute('tabindex','0');el.setAttribute('aria-label',title);el.innerHTML=svg;el.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();fn();});el.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();fn();}});actions.appendChild(el);};
        if(asset.origin==='admin'){evModelDecorate(item,asset);}
        else{makeAction('Edit model',editSvg,()=>window.edOpenFromAsset(id,true),false);makeAction('Delete',deleteSvg,()=>window.openDeleteModelConfirm(id),true);}
        item.append(thumb,name,actions);fragment.appendChild(item);
      }
      container.appendChild(fragment);
      if(index<ordered.length)requestAnimationFrame(appendBatch);
    };
    requestAnimationFrame(appendBatch);
  };

  // Asset panel cards are filtered once, then appended in short batches. Images use native lazy
  // decoding; remote fonts are downloaded only when their preview tile approaches visibility.
  let assetRenderToken=0,fontVisibilityObserver=null;
  window.renderFilteredEditorAssets=function(){
    const host=$('editorAssetResults');if(!host)return;
    const token=++assetRenderToken;
    if(fontVisibilityObserver){try{fontVisibilityObserver.disconnect();}catch(_e){}fontVisibilityObserver=null;}
    const all=typeof getEditorAssets==='function'?getEditorAssets():[];
    const q=String(typeof editorAssetSearchQuery!=='undefined'?editorAssetSearchQuery:'').trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
    const categoryFilter=typeof editorAssetCategoryFilter!=='undefined'?editorAssetCategoryFilter:'all';
    const assets=all.filter(a=>{
      const category=editorAssetCategory(a);
      if(categoryFilter!=='all'&&category!==categoryFilter)return false;
      const searchable=[a.name,a.description,a.keywords,a.tags,a.fontFamily,category,editorAssetLabel(a)].flat().join(' ').toLocaleLowerCase();
      return !q||searchable.includes(q);
    });
    host.replaceChildren();
    if(!assets.length){const empty=document.createElement('div');empty.className='editor-asset-empty';empty.textContent=all.length?'No matching assets found. Try another search or category.':'No admin assets published yet. Assets added in the Admin → Assets section will appear here.';host.appendChild(empty);return;}
    const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='editor-assets-grid';host.appendChild(grid);
    if(typeof IntersectionObserver==='function')fontVisibilityObserver=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>{
      for(const entry of entries){if(!entry.isIntersecting)continue;const card=entry.target,asset=card.__evv105Asset;
        if(asset&&typeof ensureAdminFontLoaded==='function')ensureAdminFontLoaded(asset);
        fontVisibilityObserver?.unobserve(card);
      }
    },{root:host,rootMargin:'100px 0px'});
    let index=0;
    const appendBatch=()=>{
      if(token!==assetRenderToken||!host.isConnected)return;
      const fragment=document.createDocumentFragment(),end=Math.min(index+18,assets.length);
      for(;index<end;index++){
        const asset=assets[index],category=editorAssetCategory(asset),src=String(asset.src||'');
        const card=document.createElement('button');card.type='button';card.className='editor-asset-card';card.__evv105Asset=asset;
        const thumb=document.createElement('span');thumb.className='editor-asset-thumb';
        const assetWidth=Number(asset.width||asset.videoWidth||asset.naturalWidth||asset.dimensions?.width||asset.metadata?.width||0);
        const assetHeight=Number(asset.height||asset.videoHeight||asset.naturalHeight||asset.dimensions?.height||asset.metadata?.height||0);
        if(assetWidth>0&&assetHeight>0)thumb.style.aspectRatio=assetWidth+' / '+assetHeight;
        const applyIntrinsicRatio=media=>{const w=Number(media?.naturalWidth||media?.videoWidth||0),h=Number(media?.naturalHeight||media?.videoHeight||0);if(w>0&&h>0)thumb.style.aspectRatio=w+' / '+h;};
        if(category==='font'){
          const preview=document.createElement('div');preview.className='editor-asset-font-preview';preview.textContent='Aa';
          preview.style.fontFamily='"'+String(asset.fontFamily||asset.name||'sans-serif').replace(/["\\]/g,'')+'",sans-serif';thumb.appendChild(preview);
          if(fontVisibilityObserver)fontVisibilityObserver.observe(card);
          else if(typeof ensureAdminFontLoaded==='function')ensureAdminFontLoaded(asset);
        }else if(category==='image'||category==='background-image'){
          if(src){const img=document.createElement('img');img.alt='';img.loading='lazy';img.decoding='async';img.draggable=false;img.addEventListener('load',()=>applyIntrinsicRatio(img),{once:true});img.src=src;thumb.appendChild(img);}else thumb.textContent='IMAGE';
        }else if(category==='video'||category==='background-video'){
          if(src){
            const video=document.createElement('video');video.className='editor-asset-video';video.muted=true;video.playsInline=true;video.preload='metadata';video.controls=false;video.tabIndex=-1;
            video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',()=>{applyIntrinsicRatio(video);try{if(Number(video.duration)>0)video.currentTime=Math.min(.05,video.duration/20);}catch(_e){}},{once:true});
            video.addEventListener('seeked',()=>{try{video.pause();}catch(_e){}},{once:true});
            video.addEventListener('error',()=>{const fallback=document.createElement('div');fallback.className='editor-asset-video';fallback.textContent='▶ VIDEO';video.replaceWith(fallback);},{once:true});
            video.src=src;thumb.appendChild(video);
          }else{const video=document.createElement('div');video.className='editor-asset-video';video.textContent='▶ VIDEO';thumb.appendChild(video);} 
        }else if(category==='audio'){
          const audio=document.createElement('div');audio.className='editor-asset-font-preview';audio.textContent='♫';thumb.appendChild(audio);
        }else thumb.textContent='MEDIA';
        const name=document.createElement('span');name.className='editor-asset-name';name.textContent=String(asset.name||'Asset');
        const label=document.createElement('span');label.className='editor-asset-category';label.textContent=String(editorAssetLabel(asset)||'Asset');
        card.append(thumb,name,label);card.addEventListener('click',()=>window.addAdminAssetToProject(asset.id));fragment.appendChild(card);
      }
      grid.appendChild(fragment);
      if(index<assets.length)requestAnimationFrame(appendBatch);
    };
    requestAnimationFrame(appendBatch);
  };

  // The existing timeline calls renderBackgroundTrack frequently via syncTimelineInnerWidth.
  // Rebuild its DOM only when clip geometry, selection, source or keyframe markers really change.
  const previousRenderBackground=window.renderBackgroundTrack;
  if(typeof previousRenderBackground==='function'&&!previousRenderBackground.__evv105Cache){
    const sourceStamp=value=>{const str=String(value||'');return str.length+':'+str.slice(0,44)+':'+str.slice(-18);};
    const getSignature=()=>{
      const pps=typeof pxPerSecond==='function'?Number(pxPerSecond()):0;
      const list=Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[];
      const selected=typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?String(selectedClip??''):'';
      const kf=typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'?String(selectedKfIdx??''):'';
      return [pps.toFixed(4),selected,kf,list.map(b=>{
        const markers=Array.isArray(b.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)?b.ethvelleMediaKeyframes:(Array.isArray(b.keyframes)?b.keyframes:[]);
        const markerStamp=markers.map(x=>String(x.t)+'/'+String(x.property||'')).join(',');
        return [b.id,Number(b.trimStart||0).toFixed(4),Number(b.trimEnd||0).toFixed(4),sourceStamp(b.src),String(b.name||''),markerStamp].join('~');
      }).join('|')].join('||');
    };
    const cached=function(){
      const track=$('track-background');
      if(!track||typeof bgHandleDragActive!=='undefined'&&bgHandleDragActive)return previousRenderBackground.apply(this,arguments);
      const signature=getSignature();
      if(track.dataset.evv105BgSignature===signature){
        const badge=$('bgLayerBadge');
        if(badge&&Array.isArray(backgrounds)&&backgrounds.length&&badge.innerHTML!==String(BG_FILLED_ICON||''))badge.innerHTML=BG_FILLED_ICON;
        return;
      }
      const result=previousRenderBackground.apply(this,arguments);
      if(track.isConnected&&!(typeof bgHandleDragActive!=='undefined'&&bgHandleDragActive))track.dataset.evv105BgSignature=signature;
      return result;
    };
    cached.__evv105Cache=true;window.renderBackgroundTrack=cached;
  }

  // During overlay drag, pack lanes once but only write row/clip geometry when the actual lane
  // assignment changes. Moving a clip horizontally within its existing lane no longer writes
  // every sibling's top/height on every pointer event.
  window.applyOverlayLanes=function(groupKey){
    const row=document.querySelector('.overlay-track-row[data-group="'+String(groupKey).replace(/"/g,'\\"')+'"]');
    if(!row)return;const track=row.querySelector('.layer-track');if(!track)return;
    const groupClips=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).filter(o=>ethvelleOverlayGroupKey(o)===groupKey);
    const lanes=typeof packIntoLanes==='function'?packIntoLanes(groupClips):[];
    const laneCount=Math.max(1,lanes.length),rowH=overlayRowHeightForLanes(laneCount);
    const signature=lanes.map((lane,i)=>i+':'+lane.map(o=>String(o.id)).join(',')).join('|');
    if(track.dataset.evv105LaneSignature===signature&&Math.round(parseFloat(track.style.height)||0)===Math.round(rowH)&&Math.round(parseFloat(row.style.height)||0)===Math.round(rowH+4))return;
    const setIfChanged=(el,prop,value)=>{if(el.style[prop]!==value)el.style[prop]=value;};
    setIfChanged(row,'height',(rowH+4)+'px');setIfChanged(row,'minHeight',(rowH+4)+'px');setIfChanged(row,'maxHeight','none');
    setIfChanged(track,'height',rowH+'px');setIfChanged(track,'minHeight',rowH+'px');setIfChanged(track,'maxHeight',rowH+'px');
    lanes.forEach((lane,laneIdx)=>lane.forEach(ov=>{
      const el=track.querySelector('.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id="'+String(ov.id).replace(/"/g,'\\"')+'"]');
      if(el)setIfChanged(el,'top',overlayLaneTop(laneIdx)+'px');
    }));
    track.dataset.evv105LaneSignature=signature;
  };

  // Promote only the currently selected large media surface while the user is manipulating it;
  // release the GPU hint shortly after the gesture so inactive 720p/1080p media does not all
  // retain its own compositor surface. No source pixels or export dimensions are altered.
  let promotedMedia=null,demoteTimer=0;
  function selectedMediaElement(){
    try{
      const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');
      const bg=Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds.find(x=>String(x.id)===id):null;
      if(bg)return $('bgImgLayer');
      const ov=Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(x=>String(x.id)===id&&(x.type==='image'||x.type==='video')):null;
      if(!ov)return null;
      const node=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[ov.id]:null;
      return node||document.querySelector('.overlay-media-item[data-overlay-id="'+id.replace(/"/g,'\\"')+'"] img, .overlay-media-item[data-overlay-id="'+id.replace(/"/g,'\\"')+'"] video');
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function promoteMedia(){
    if(demoteTimer){clearTimeout(demoteTimer);demoteTimer=0;}
    const el=selectedMediaElement();if(!el)return;
    if(promotedMedia&&promotedMedia!==el)promotedMedia.style.willChange='auto';
    promotedMedia=el;el.style.willChange='transform, clip-path';
  }
  function scheduleDemote(){
    if(demoteTimer)clearTimeout(demoteTimer);
    demoteTimer=setTimeout(()=>{demoteTimer=0;if(promotedMedia){promotedMedia.style.willChange='auto';promotedMedia=null;}},130);
  }
  const stage=$('canvasStage');if(stage){stage.addEventListener('pointerdown',promoteMedia,{capture:true,passive:true});stage.addEventListener('touchstart',promoteMedia,{capture:true,passive:true});}
  const toolPanel=$('toolPanel');if(toolPanel){toolPanel.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(e.target?.matches?.('input[type="range"]'))promoteMedia();},{capture:true,passive:true});toolPanel.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target?.matches?.('input[type="range"]'))promoteMedia();},{passive:true});}
  window.addEventListener('pointerup',scheduleDemote,{capture:true,passive:true});window.addEventListener('pointercancel',scheduleDemote,{capture:true,passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('blur',scheduleDemote);
})();
</script>


<!-- Ethvelle v106: keep clip movement on the compositor; no per-frame row reflow. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v106-smooth-drag-css">
  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner .anim-clip.dragging.evv-timeline-transform-dragging,
  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner .track-clip.dragging.evv-timeline-transform-dragging,
  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner .overlay-clip.dragging.evv-timeline-transform-dragging{
    will-change:transform!important;
    transition:none!important;
    backface-visibility:hidden!important;
    -webkit-backface-visibility:hidden!important;
  }
  /* A body drag is a pure translation. Lane/row geometry is reconciled only at release. */
  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner .overlay-clip.dragging.evv-timeline-transform-dragging{
    transition:none!important;
  }
</style>


<!-- Ethvelle v107: smoother canvas/timeline gestures, crisp drag rendering and full text-plate bounds. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v107-performance-fixes-css">
  /* Keep the character badge centered vertically. When its clip's left selection tab
     overlaps it, nudge the badge only enough to clear the tab (not off the row). */
  #screen-editor #timelineInner #characterRows .layer-row.char-row .layer-badge{
    /* Default/resting icon sits 2px higher. Selection keeps the exact same vertical position. */
    margin-top:7px!important;
    z-index:8!important;
    transform:translateY(-2px)!important;
    transition:none!important;
  }
  
  

  /* Keep compositor movement, but avoid the low-quality back-face raster path on active clips. */
  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner .anim-clip.dragging.evv-timeline-transform-dragging,
  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner .track-clip.dragging.evv-timeline-transform-dragging,
  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner .overlay-clip.dragging.evv-timeline-transform-dragging{
    will-change:transform!important;
    transition:none!important;
    backface-visibility:visible!important;
    -webkit-backface-visibility:visible!important;
    transform-style:flat!important;
  }
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v107-performance-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';

  /* The visible background plate is a ::before pseudo-element extending `padding`
     pixels beyond the text node. The original text selection bounds measured only the
     node itself; v54 already adds textHighlightPadding, so apply only the missing delta. */
  const previousSelBoxTarget=window.selBoxTarget;
  if(typeof previousSelBoxTarget==='function'&&!previousSelBoxTarget.__evv107TextPlateBounds){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const target=previousSelBoxTarget.apply(this,arguments);
      if(!target||target.kind!=='overlay')return target;
      let list=[];
      try{list=Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:(typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:[]);}catch(_e){}
      const id=String(window.selectedClip??(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?selectedClip:''));
      const model=list.find(item=>item&&item.type==='text'&&String(item.id)===id);
      if(!model)return target;
      const plateEnabled=!!(model.textBackgroundEnabled||model.textHighlightEnabled);
      const bgPadding=model.textBackgroundPadding;
      const highlightPadding=model.textHighlightPadding;
      const platePadding=plateEnabled?Math.max(0,Number(bgPadding==null?(highlightPadding==null?8:highlightPadding):bgPadding)||0):0;
      const alreadyIncluded=model.textHighlightEnabled?Math.max(0,Number(highlightPadding)||0):0;
      const correction=platePadding-alreadyIncluded;
      if(Math.abs(correction)>.01){
        target.w0=Math.max(34,(Number(target.w0)||34)+2*correction);
        target.h0=Math.max(30,(Number(target.h0)||30)+2*correction);
      }
      return target;
    };
    wrapped.__evv107TextPlateBounds=true;
    window.selBoxTarget=wrapped;
  }

  /* Padding/background controls update the text node live. Reposition the white selection
     frame in the same display frame so it grows/shrinks with the visible background plate. */
  const boundsKeys=new Set([
    'textBackgroundEnabled','textBackgroundPadding',
    'textHighlightEnabled','textHighlightPadding'
  ]);
  let boundsRefreshRaf=0;
  function scheduleTextBoundsRefresh(key){
    if(!boundsKeys.has(String(key))||boundsRefreshRaf)return;
    boundsRefreshRaf=requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      boundsRefreshRaf=0;
      if(typeof window.updateSelBox==='function')window.updateSelBox();
    });
  }
  function wrapBoundsSetter(name){
    const previous=window[name];
    if(typeof previous!=='function'||previous.__evv107BoundsRefresh)return;
    const wrapped=function(key){
      const result=previous.apply(this,arguments);
      scheduleTextBoundsRefresh(key);
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv107BoundsRefresh=true;
    window[name]=wrapped;
  }
  wrapBoundsSetter('ethvellePicsartSetValue');
  wrapBoundsSetter('ethvelleTextStyleChanged');
})();
</script>

  }

  }
  #screen-editor #toolPanel .evtr-ruler{touch-action:none!important;}

<script id="ethvelle-v110-instant-touch-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
  const clock=()=>((window.performance&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now());

  /* 1. Native-feeling bottom toolbar swipe: begin after a normal 6px move, never a long press. */
  const toolbarStates=new WeakMap();
  function bindToolbar(row){
    if(!row||row.dataset.evv110TouchScroll==='1')return;
    row.dataset.evv110TouchScroll='1';
    row.style.setProperty('touch-action','pan-y','important');
    const state={id:null,mode:'idle',x:0,y:0,lastX:0,lastAt:0,startScroll:0,targetScroll:0,maxScroll:0,velocity:0,moveRaf:0,glideRaf:0,suppressUntil:0};
    toolbarStates.set(row,state);
    const stopGlide=()=>{if(state.glideRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(state.glideRaf);state.glideRaf=0;}state.velocity=0;};
    const flush=()=>{
      state.moveRaf=0;
      if(state.mode==='drag')row.scrollLeft=clamp(state.targetScroll,0,state.maxScroll);
    };
    const glide=()=>{
      if(Math.abs(state.velocity)<0.07)return;
      let previous=clock();
      const tick=()=>{
        const t=clock(),dt=Math.min(32,Math.max(1,t-previous));previous=t;
        const next=clamp(row.scrollLeft+state.velocity*dt,0,state.maxScroll);
        row.scrollLeft=next;
        state.velocity*=Math.pow(0.925,dt/16.667);
        if(next<=0||next>=state.maxScroll||Math.abs(state.velocity)<0.035){state.glideRaf=0;state.velocity=0;return;}
        state.glideRaf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      };
      state.glideRaf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    row.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      if(e.isPrimary===false||(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0))return;
      // Geometry is stable for the duration of one swipe. Read the scroll range once here,
      // instead of asking layout for scrollWidth/clientWidth on every RAF flush and glide tick.
      state.maxScroll=Math.max(0,row.scrollWidth-row.clientWidth);
      if(state.maxScroll<=2)return;
      stopGlide();
      state.id=e.pointerId;state.mode='pending';state.x=state.lastX=e.clientX;state.y=e.clientY;
      state.lastAt=clock();state.startScroll=row.scrollLeft;state.targetScroll=row.scrollLeft;state.velocity=0;
    },{capture:true,passive:true});
    row.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      if(state.id!==e.pointerId)return;
      const dx=e.clientX-state.x,dy=e.clientY-state.y;
      if(state.mode==='pending'){
        if(Math.max(Math.abs(dx),Math.abs(dy))<6)return;
        if(Math.abs(dy)>Math.abs(dx)*1.12){
          // Keep the pointer identity until pointerup so the generated click from a vertical
          // swipe that starts on a tool cannot activate that tool. Clearing state.id here
          // made pointerup invisible to this controller and let the synthetic click through.
          state.mode='cancelled';
          state.suppressUntil=clock()+500;
          return;
        }
        if(Math.abs(dx)<6)return;
        state.mode='drag';
        state.suppressUntil=clock()+260;
        try{row.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
      }
      if(state.mode!=='drag')return;
      const t=clock(),dt=Math.max(1,t-state.lastAt),deltaX=e.clientX-state.lastX;
      const instant=-deltaX/dt;
      state.velocity=clamp(state.velocity*.32+instant*.68,-2.4,2.4);
      state.lastX=e.clientX;state.lastAt=t;
      state.targetScroll=state.startScroll-dx;
      if(!state.moveRaf)state.moveRaf=requestAnimationFrame(flush);
      if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
    },{passive:false});
    row.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{
      if(state.id!==e.pointerId)return;
      const dragged=state.mode==='drag';
      if(state.moveRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(state.moveRaf);state.moveRaf=0;flush();}
      state.id=null;state.mode='idle';
      if(dragged){state.suppressUntil=clock()+260;try{row.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}glide();}
    },{passive:true});
    row.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>{
      if(state.id!==e.pointerId)return;
      state.id=null;state.mode='idle';state.velocity=0;state.suppressUntil=0;
      if(state.moveRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(state.moveRaf);state.moveRaf=0;}
    },{passive:true});
    row.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      if(!state.suppressUntil)return;
      if(clock()>state.suppressUntil){state.suppressUntil=0;return;}
      state.suppressUntil=0;e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    },true);
  }
  ['bottomToolsDefault','bottomToolsClip','bottomToolsKeyframe'].forEach(id=>bindToolbar($(id)));

  /* 2. Hidden Transform ruler: require a grab on its centre marker, not a random rail tap. */
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const target=e.target;
    const ruler=target&&target.closest?target.closest('#screen-editor #toolPanel .evtr-ruler'):null;
    if(ruler){
      const r=ruler.getBoundingClientRect();
      // The transform ruler has a fixed blue centre marker; only that marker can start a drag.
      if(Math.abs(e.clientX-(r.left+r.width/2))>13||Math.abs(e.clientY-(r.top+r.height/2))>Math.min(24,r.height/2+1)){
        if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      }
    }
  },{capture:true,passive:false});

  /* 3. Finish clip-row scrolling with gentle inertia after the direct RAF handoff. */
  let clipGlideRaf=0,clipGlideGeneration=0;
  function stopClipGlide(){clipGlideGeneration++;if(clipGlideRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(clipGlideRaf);clipGlideRaf=0;}}
  function startClipGlide(){
    const generation=++clipGlideGeneration;
    const motion=window.__ethvelleV110ClipScrollMotion;
    if(!motion||!motion.scroller||!motion.scroller.isConnected)return;
    motion.active=false;
    let velocity=clamp(Number(motion.velocity)||0,-2.4,2.4);
    const scroller=motion.scroller;
    // Flush the last finger position first; the original helper may have one RAF queued.
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      if(generation!==clipGlideGeneration)return;
      if(Number.isFinite(motion.targetTop))scroller.scrollTop=clamp(motion.targetTop,0,Math.max(0,scroller.scrollHeight-scroller.clientHeight));
      if(Math.abs(velocity)<0.07)return;
      let previous=clock();
      const tick=()=>{
        const t=clock(),dt=Math.min(32,Math.max(1,t-previous));previous=t;
        const max=Math.max(0,scroller.scrollHeight-scroller.clientHeight);
        const next=clamp(scroller.scrollTop+velocity*dt,0,max);
        scroller.scrollTop=next;
        velocity*=Math.pow(0.93,dt/16.667);
        if(next<=0||next>=max||Math.abs(velocity)<0.035){clipGlideRaf=0;return;}
        clipGlideRaf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      };
      clipGlideRaf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    });
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    if(e.target?.closest?.('#screen-editor #timelineScroll')){
      stopClipGlide();
      const m=window.__ethvelleV110ClipScrollMotion;if(m)m.active=false;
    }
  },{capture:true,passive:true});
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>{
    const m=window.__ethvelleV110ClipScrollMotion;
    if(m&&m.active)startClipGlide();
  },{capture:true,passive:true});
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{
    const m=window.__ethvelleV110ClipScrollMotion;if(m)m.active=false;
    stopClipGlide();
  },{capture:true,passive:true});

  /* 4. Selection-outline X: block the legacy pointer/click delete handlers, then deselect. */
  const closeButton=$('sbDelete');
  if(closeButton){
    closeButton.title='Deselect layer';closeButton.setAttribute('aria-label','Deselect layer');
    closeButton.setAttribute('role','button');closeButton.tabIndex=0;
    closeButton.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation();},true);
    const deselect=e=>{
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      if(typeof window.deselectAllClips==='function')window.deselectAllClips();
      else if(typeof deselectAllClips==='function')deselectAllClips();
    };
    closeButton.addEventListener('click',deselect,true);
    closeButton.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){deselect(e);}},true);
  }
})();
</script>

<style id="ethvelle-v110-keyframe-toolbar-options-css">
  
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #keyframeMediaAccessTool,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #evcaFrameCaptureTool-keyframe,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #keyframeDoneTool,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe #keyframeDeleteTool{
    flex:0 0 25%!important;width:25%!important;min-width:0!important;max-width:25%!important;
  }
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe .evv-kf-mirror{
    display:flex!important;flex:0 0 auto!important;flex-shrink:0!important;
    min-width:68px!important;max-width:none!important;width:auto!important;
    align-items:center!important;justify-content:center!important;text-align:center!important;
    padding:10px 7px 8px!important;gap:5px!important;cursor:pointer!important;
  }
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe .evv-kf-mirror *{pointer-events:none!important;}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v110-keyframe-toolbar-options-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const clipRow=document.getElementById('bottomToolsClip');
  const keyRow=document.getElementById('bottomToolsKeyframe');
  if(!clipRow||!keyRow)return;
  const duplicateControls=new Set([
    'clipMediaAccessTool','evcaFrameCaptureTool-clip','evv72SaveFrame-clip','evv72AddFrame-clip'
  ]);
  let lastSignature='';

  function removeMirrors(){
    keyRow.querySelectorAll('.evv-kf-mirror').forEach(el=>el.remove());
    lastSignature='';
  }
  function sourceTools(){
    return Array.from(clipRow.children).filter(el=>{
      if(!el.classList?.contains('tool-item'))return false;
      if(duplicateControls.has(el.id))return false;
      if(el.id==='clipDeleteCharacterTool')return false; // No whole-character Delete in keyframe mode.
      const label=(el.querySelector('span:last-child')?.textContent||'').trim().toLowerCase();
      if(label==='delete')return false;                 // No normal selected-layer/clip Delete in keyframe mode.
      if(label==='done')return false;
      return getComputedStyle(el).display!=='none';
    });
  }
  function buildMirrors(){
    const selected=typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'&&selectedKfIdx!==null;
    const ref=typeof selectedClip==='undefined'?'':String(selectedClip||'');
    const isCharacterKeyframe=selected&&(ref==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(ref));
    if(!isCharacterKeyframe){removeMirrors();return;}

    // Recompute the normal character-row visibility using the app's existing sync logic,
    // then immediately restore the selected-keyframe row before the browser paints.
    if(typeof oldSync==='function'){
      const savedKfIndex=selectedKfIdx;
      selectedKfIdx=null;
      try{oldSync.apply(window,[]);}finally{selectedKfIdx=savedKfIndex;}
      const defaultRow=document.getElementById('bottomToolsDefault');
      [defaultRow,clipRow].forEach(row=>row?.style.setProperty('display','none','important'));
      keyRow.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
    }

    const sources=sourceTools();
    // Keep the existing Add model action available while the two Delete actions are hidden.
    const addCharacterTool=document.querySelector('#bottomToolsDefault [data-tool="model"]');
    if(addCharacterTool)sources.unshift(addCharacterTool);
    const signature=sources.map(el=>(el.id||'')+':'+(el.textContent||'').trim()).join('|');
    if(signature===lastSignature&&keyRow.querySelectorAll('.evv-kf-mirror').length===sources.length)return;
    removeMirrors();
    sources.forEach(source=>{
      const proxy=source.cloneNode(true);
      proxy.removeAttribute('id');
      proxy.classList.add('evv-kf-mirror');
      proxy.setAttribute('role','button');
      proxy.setAttribute('tabindex','0');
      proxy.setAttribute('aria-label',(source.getAttribute('aria-label')||(source.textContent||'').trim()||'Character tool').trim());
      Array.from(proxy.attributes||[]).forEach(attr=>{if(/^on/i.test(attr.name))proxy.removeAttribute(attr.name);});
      proxy.querySelectorAll('[id]').forEach(node=>node.removeAttribute('id'));
      proxy.querySelectorAll('*').forEach(node=>{
        Array.from(node.attributes||[]).forEach(attr=>{if(/^on/i.test(attr.name))node.removeAttribute(attr.name);});
      });
      proxy.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
      const activate=e=>{
        if(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();}
        source.click();
      };
      proxy.addEventListener('click',activate);
      proxy.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){activate(e);}});
      keyRow.appendChild(proxy);
    });
    lastSignature=signature;
  }

  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__ethvelleV110KeyframeTools){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);
      buildMirrors();
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__ethvelleV110KeyframeTools=true;
    window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  // This is an initialization-only fallback; later keyframe selections flow through the wrapper above.
  buildMirrors();
})();
</script>

  /* Character rows now grow to fit their actual number of overlap lanes. CSS custom
     properties are set by the renderer so the existing fixed-height !important rules
     elsewhere in the file cannot clamp a multi-lane row back to one lane. */
  #timelineInner .layer-row.char-row {
    height:var(--ev-char-row-height,46px)!important;
    min-height:var(--ev-char-row-height,46px)!important;
    max-height:var(--ev-char-row-height,46px)!important;
    align-items:center!important;
    overflow:hidden!important;
  }
  #timelineInner .layer-row.char-row .char-row-track {
    height:var(--ev-char-track-height,42px)!important;
    min-height:var(--ev-char-track-height,42px)!important;
    max-height:var(--ev-char-track-height,42px)!important;
    margin:0!important;
    align-self:center!important;
    overflow:hidden!important;
  }
  #timelineInner .layer-row.char-row .char-row-track .anim-clip,
  #timelineInner .layer-row.char-row .char-row-track .anim-clip.selected,
  #timelineInner .layer-row.char-row .char-row-track .anim-clip.dragging,

  }

<style id="ethvelle-v113-targeted-performance-and-billing-css">
/* Consolidated selected-clip relational selectors: one pass instead of repeated version blocks. */
#timelineInner .layer-row:has(.anim-clip.selected),

<script id="ethvelle-v114-requested-ui-updates-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const panel=document.getElementById('toolPanel');
  const editor=document.getElementById('screen-editor');
  if(!panel||!editor)return;

  function fitAssetsPanel(){
    if(!panel.classList.contains('open')||!panel.classList.contains('ev-v114-assets-fit'))return;
    const canvas=editor.querySelector('.canvas-wrap');
    if(!canvas)return;
    const screenRect=editor.getBoundingClientRect();
    const canvasRect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const top=Math.max(0,Math.min(screenRect.height-150,canvasRect.bottom-screenRect.top+1));
    const height=Math.max(150,screenRect.height-top);
    panel.style.setProperty('--ev-v114-assets-top',top+'px');
    panel.style.setProperty('--ev-v114-assets-height',height+'px');
  }

  const originalOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof originalOpen==='function'&&!originalOpen.__evV114AssetsFit){
    const wrappedOpen=function(key){
      const result=originalOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      panel.classList.toggle('ev-v114-assets-fit',key==='assets');
      if(key==='assets'){
        requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(fitAssetsPanel));
      }else{
        panel.style.removeProperty('--ev-v114-assets-top');
        panel.style.removeProperty('--ev-v114-assets-height');
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedOpen.__evV114AssetsFit=true;
    window.openPanel=wrappedOpen;
  }

  const originalClose=window.closePanel;
  if(typeof originalClose==='function'&&!originalClose.__evV114AssetsFit){
    const wrappedClose=function(){
      const result=originalClose.apply(this,arguments);
      panel.classList.remove('ev-v114-assets-fit');
      panel.style.removeProperty('--ev-v114-assets-top');
      panel.style.removeProperty('--ev-v114-assets-height');
      return result;
    };
    wrappedClose.__evV114AssetsFit=true;
    window.closePanel=wrappedClose;
  }

  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>requestAnimationFrame(fitAssetsPanel),{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport){
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',()=>requestAnimationFrame(fitAssetsPanel),{passive:true});
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v115-filter-click-state-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const original=window.setEditorAssetCategory;
  if(typeof original==='function'&&!original.__evV115ClickState){
    const wrapped=function(category){
      const result=original.apply(this,arguments);
      document.querySelectorAll('#editorAssetFilters .editor-asset-filter').forEach(btn=>{
        btn.classList.toggle('user-selected',btn.dataset.filter===String(category));
      });
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV115ClickState=true;
    window.setEditorAssetCategory=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v119-face-gesture-runtime">
(function ethvelleV119Enhancements(){
  'use strict';

  // All legacy presets are upgraded to the 21-bone pose contract used by the newer
  // cut-out renderer. applyPoseToBones converts these poses back for older legacy rigs.
  try{
    if(typeof animPresets==='object' && typeof ethvelleNormalizeLegacyPoseTo21==='function'){
      Object.keys(animPresets).forEach(key=>{
        const preset=animPresets[key];
        if(!preset || preset._ethvelleRigVersion===21 || !Array.isArray(preset.keyframes))return;
        preset.keyframes=preset.keyframes.map(kf=>({...kf,pose:ethvelleNormalizeLegacyPoseTo21(kf.pose||{})}));
        preset._ethvelleRigVersion=21;
      });
    }
  }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle: legacy preset compatibility pass skipped',err);}

  const validMood=new Set(['sad','angry','shock','laugh']);
  const validHand=new Set(['none','point','thumb','palm','fist','hold']);
  function selectedStickmanClip(){
    if(typeof getClipByRef!=='function')return null;
    if(selectedClip==='main' || (typeof selectedClip==='string' && /^seg\d+$/.test(selectedClip)))return getClipByRef(selectedClip);
    return null;
  }
  function activeActionCharacter(){return typeof activeCharacter==='function'?activeCharacter():null;}
  function actionDefaults(){return {expression:'none',mouth:'preset',blink:'auto',eyes:'auto',leftHand:'none',rightHand:'none',wings:'natural'};}
  function normalizeActions(input){
    const d=actionDefaults(),a={...d,...(input&&typeof input==='object'?input:{})};
    if(!['none',...validMood].includes(a.expression))a.expression='none';
    if(!['preset','talk','closed','hold'].includes(a.mouth))a.mouth='preset';
    if(!['auto','ambient','off'].includes(a.blink))a.blink='auto';
    if(!['auto','open','closed'].includes(a.eyes))a.eyes='auto';
    if(!validHand.has(a.leftHand))a.leftHand='none';
    if(!validHand.has(a.rightHand))a.rightHand='none';
    if(!['natural','fly'].includes(a.wings))a.wings='natural';
    return a;
  }
  function actionOption(value,label,current){return `<option value="${value}"${value===current?' selected':''}>${label}</option>`;}
  function handOptions(side,current,parts){
    let html=actionOption('none','Natural hand',current);
    [['point','Pointing'],['thumb','Thumbs up'],['palm','Open palm'],['fist','Fist'],['hold','Holding grip']].forEach(([key,label])=>{
      if(parts && parts['hand'+side+'_'+key])html+=actionOption(key,label,current);
    });
    return html;
  }
  function ensureActionsBackdrop(){
    let el=document.getElementById('ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop');
    if(el)return el;
    const host=document.getElementById('screen-editor')||document.getElementById('app')||document.body;
    el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop';el.className='modal-backdrop evca-char-actions-backdrop';
    el.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===el)closeClipCharacterActions();});
    host.appendChild(el);return el;
  }
  function openClipCharacterActions(){
    const clip=selectedStickmanClip();
    if(!clip){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select an animation clip first',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1700});return;}
    const character=activeActionCharacter(),parts=character&&character.sheet&&character.sheet.parts||{};
    if(!character){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a character first',{duration:1600});return;}
    clip.faceActions=normalizeActions(clip.faceActions);
    const a=clip.faceActions,modelType=String(character.modelType||(character.sheet&&character.sheet.modelType)||'humanoid');
    const isBirdKit=modelType==='bird'&&!!(parts.bird_eye&&parts.bird_brow&&parts.beak_closed&&parts.beak_open);
    const isHumanoidKit=modelType==='humanoid'&&!!(parts.mouth_inner&&parts.eye_L&&parts.eye_R&&parts.brow_L&&parts.brow_R);
    const hasFace=!!(parts.head_blink||parts.head_sad||parts.head_angry||parts.head_shock||parts.head_laugh||parts.mouth_open||isBirdKit||isHumanoidKit);
    const backdrop=ensureActionsBackdrop();
    const faceOptions=actionOption('none','Keep original',a.expression)+
      ((parts.head_sad||isBirdKit)?actionOption('sad','Sad',a.expression):'')+
      ((parts.head_angry||isBirdKit)?actionOption('angry','Angry',a.expression):'')+
      ((parts.head_shock||isBirdKit)?actionOption('shock','Surprised',a.expression):'')+
      ((parts.head_laugh||isBirdKit)?actionOption('laugh','Laughing',a.expression):'');
    const hasAnimalFaceTalk=modelType==='animal'&&!!(parts.mouth_inner&&parts.head_laugh);
    const hasHumanoidFaceTalk=isHumanoidKit&&!!parts.head_laugh;
    const hasBirdBeakTalk=isBirdKit;
    const mouthOptions=actionOption('preset','Use animation mouth',a.mouth)+
      ((parts.mouth_open||hasAnimalFaceTalk||hasHumanoidFaceTalk||hasBirdBeakTalk)?actionOption('talk','Talking loop',a.mouth):'')+
      actionOption('closed','Keep mouth closed',a.mouth)+
      ((modelType==='animal'||modelType==='bird')?actionOption('hold','Hold item',a.mouth):'');
    const blinkOptions=actionOption('auto','Blink through this clip',a.blink)+actionOption('ambient','Use natural ambient blinking',a.blink)+actionOption('off','Turn blinking off',a.blink);
    const wingControl=isBirdKit?`<div class="evca-face-actions-field full"><label for="evcaActionWings">Wing style</label><select id="evcaActionWings" onchange="updateClipFaceAction('wings',this.value)">${actionOption('natural','Default wings',a.wings)}${actionOption('fly','Flying wings',a.wings)}</select></div>`:'';
    backdrop.innerHTML=`<div class="modal-sheet evca-char-actions-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Face and hand actions">
      <div class="sheet-handle"></div><p class="sheet-title">Face &amp; hand actions</p>
      <p class="sheet-sub">These actions are attached to the selected animation clip, not the whole character.</p>
      <div class="evca-face-actions-grid">
        <div class="evca-face-actions-field full"><label for="evcaActionExpression">Facial expression</label><select id="evcaActionExpression" onchange="updateClipFaceAction('expression',this.value)">${faceOptions}</select></div>
        <div class="evca-face-actions-field"><label for="evcaActionMouth">Mouth movement</label><select id="evcaActionMouth" onchange="updateClipFaceAction('mouth',this.value)">${mouthOptions}</select></div>
        <div class="evca-face-actions-field"><label for="evcaActionBlink">Eye blinking</label><select id="evcaActionBlink" onchange="updateClipFaceAction('blink',this.value)">${blinkOptions}</select></div>
        ${wingControl}
        <div class="evca-face-actions-field"><label for="evcaActionLeftHand">Left hand gesture</label><select id="evcaActionLeftHand" onchange="updateClipFaceAction('leftHand',this.value)">${handOptions('L',a.leftHand,parts)}</select></div>
        <div class="evca-face-actions-field"><label for="evcaActionRightHand">Right hand gesture</label><select id="evcaActionRightHand" onchange="updateClipFaceAction('rightHand',this.value)">${handOptions('R',a.rightHand,parts)}</select></div>
      </div>
      <p class="evca-face-actions-note">Expression, mouth and hand options use the matching pieces from your 14-part base sheet and 13-part expression/gesture sheet. Missing pieces are not offered.</p>
      <button type="button" class="evca-face-actions-save" onclick="closeClipCharacterActions()">Done</button>
    </div>`;
    backdrop.classList.add('active');
  }
  function closeClipCharacterActions(){
    const el=document.getElementById('ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop');
    if(el)el.classList.remove('active');
  }
  function updateClipFaceAction(field,value){
    const clip=selectedStickmanClip(),character=activeActionCharacter();
    if(!clip || !character)return;
    const a=normalizeActions(clip.faceActions);
    if(field==='expression' && (value==='none'||validMood.has(value)))a.expression=value;
    else if(field==='mouth' && ['preset','talk','closed','hold'].includes(value))a.mouth=value;
    else if(field==='blink' && ['auto','ambient','off'].includes(value))a.blink=value;
    else if(field==='leftHand' && validHand.has(value))a.leftHand=value;
    else if(field==='rightHand' && validHand.has(value))a.rightHand=value;
    else if(field==='wings' && ['natural','fly'].includes(value))a.wings=value;
    clip.faceActions=a;
